| AOE-2837 | power | Mains inputs: 120 Vrms (North America and a few others), 220 or 240 Vrms elsewhere; offline switchers rectify the line directly to ~160 V dc or ~320 V dc and get galvanic isolation from a high-frequency (20 kHz-1 MHz) transformer; linear supplies use a line-frequency transformer that both isolates and transforms. | V_dc(rectified) ~ 160 V (120 Vac) / ~320 V (230-240 Vac) | line standard | Offline supply architecture (Fig. 9.48) | review | p.629 §9.5, footnote 38 | high |
| AOE-2838 | compliance | Always use a 3-wire line cord with the protective-earth (green or green/yellow) conductor bonded to the instrument case, so an insulation failure or line-to-case contact blows a fuse instead of making the case lethal; prefer an IEC chassis inlet or a power-entry module (IEC inlet + fuse holder + line filter + switch). Colors: US black = hot/line, white = neutral, green = ground; IEC brown = line, blue = neutral, green/yellow = ground. | PE bonded to chassis | line-cord type | All mains-powered equipment | inspect | p.629 §9.5.1A, footnote 39 | high |

| AOE-2841 | protection | Use a slow-blow mains fuse (turn-on inrush from charging filter capacitors). | AOE-2842 | compliance | Wire cartridge fuse holders (3AG/AGC/MDL) with the hot (line) lead to the rear terminal so nobody changing the fuse can touch the powerline; power-entry modules prevent fuse access without removing the line cord. | hot -> rear terminal | fuse-holder wiring | Panel fuse holders | inspect | p.631 §9.5.1B(c) | high |
| AOE-2843 | emc | Put a line-rated (X1/X2) capacitor across a front-panel mains switch to prevent arcing, and give the transformer primary some bridging capacitance (a line filter in the entry module can provide it). | X-class capacitor across switch contacts | switch location | Mains switches | inspect | p.631 §9.5.1C | high |
| AOE-2844 | emc | Include a mains lowpass (RFI) filter - an LC pi-section with a coupled (common-mode) inductor pair - to stop RFI leaving via the powerline and to reject incoming interference; with transient suppressors it also guards against the 1-5 kV spikes occasionally present on most power lines. Distrust filter attenuation curves: they are specified with 50 ohm source and load, unlike real lines. | spikes 1-5 kV occasional; datasheet attenuation @ 50 ohm/50 ohm only | filter datasheet | Mains inputs | review | p.631 §9.5.1D, footnote 41 | high |
| AOE-2845 | compliance | Use only line-rated, self-healing safety capacitors on the mains: X class (X1-X3) across the line where failure is not a shock hazard (common X2: 250 Vac, 1.2 kV peak); Y class (Y1-Y4) from line to ground where failure would be a shock hazard (common Y2: 250 Vac, 5 kV peak). | X2: 250 Vac / 1.2 kV pk; Y2: 250 Vac / 5 kV pk | position (L-N vs L-PE) | Mains filters and bypassing (Fig. 9.49, 9.50) | inspect | p.631 §9.5.1E, footnotes 42-43 | high |
| AOE-2846 | compliance | Bleed the across-the-line X capacitor: after unplugging it can hold the peak line voltage (up to 325 V) across the exposed plug pins; a parallel discharge resistor must give a time constant < 1 s (Qualtek filter module 1 M; an Astrodyne switcher 540 k). The bleeder dissipates continuously (540 k at 220 Vac = 100 mW), a significant standby loss for Energy Star designs; an X-cap discharge IC (Power Integrations CAPZero) switches the resistors in only when no line reversal is seen for 20 ms. | tau = R_bleed*C_X < 1 s; P_bleed = V_rms^2/R_bleed | C_X (F), R_bleed (ohm), V_line (Vrms) | Mains inputs with X capacitors | calc | p.631-632 §9.5.1E, footnote 44 | high |
| AOE-2847 | protection | Discharge large high-voltage dc storage capacitors at power-off (an ac-sensing X-cap discharger will not do): use normally-closed relay contacts held open while ac is present, or a high-voltage depletion-mode MOSFET driven by a photovoltaic stack. | discharge path active when ac absent | C (F), V (V) | HV dc links | review | p.632 §9.5.1E | high |
| AOE-2848 | protection | Fit a mains transient suppressor (MOV; shunts hundreds of amps of spike current) with a thermal-cutout fuse, which protects against an MOV that conducts partially (sustained high line, or an aged MOV whose breakdown voltage has dropped after absorbing large transients). | MOV + thermal fuse | line transient environment | Mains inputs (Fig. 9.49) | inspect | p.632 §9.5.1F | high |
| AOE-2849 | compliance | Insulate every exposed line-voltage connection inside an instrument (heat-shrink tubing, not friction or electrical tape); the front-panel ON-OFF switch near low-voltage wiring is especially hazardous during test. | qualitative | - | Mains wiring inside products | inspect | p.632 §9.5.1G | high |
| AOE-2850 | compliance | Never run an instrument from the powerline without an isolating transformer (or isolated converter): transformerless supplies put the circuit at line potential relative to external ground and have no place in equipment that interconnects with other equipment; take extreme care servicing such gear (a grounded scope probe on its chassis can shock or short). | galvanic isolation required for interconnectable equipment | architecture | Offline supplies (non-isolated only in self-contained products like LED bulbs, clocks, smoke alarms) | review | p.632 §9.5.2, footnote 45 | high |
| AOE-2851 | power | Keep the unregulated dc as low as possible (pass-transistor dissipation depends on its average) but make certain its minimum (ripple trough) never falls below the regulator's minimum input - typically V_out + 2 V (LM317 class) or + 0.5-1 V (LDO) - at low line (10% below nominal, 105 Vac US) or even brownout (20% below nominal); otherwise 120 Hz dips appear on the output. | V_unreg,min(trough @ line -10% or -20%) >= V_out + V_do; example +5 V reg: +10 V at trough with 1-2 Vpp ripple | V_rms,sec, line tolerance, ripple (V) | Linear supplies from transformer-rectifier-capacitor | calc | p.632-633 §9.5.2 | high |
| AOE-2852 | power | Estimate bridge-rectifier dc from the secondary rating (peak ~ 1.4 x Vrms minus two diode drops) but measure it under worst case (full load, low line 105 V) when the regulator runs near minimum headroom, because winding resistance and leakage inductance lower the loaded output; use a primary-tapped transformer (e.g. Triad F-90X) for final adjustment. Example: +/-50 V at full load needs ~52 V peak (~37 Vrms) - a 40 Vrms unit was chosen to cover the loaded drop. | V_pk ~ 1.4*V_rms - 2*V_D | V_rms (V), V_D (V) | Transformer selection (Fig. 9.49) | measure | p.633 §9.5.2 | high |
| AOE-2853 | components | Allow for loose electrolytic filter-capacitor tolerance: -30% to +100% about nominal is not unusual (the example parts are +/-20%). | C_min = 0.7 x C_nom (worst case) | tolerance | Ripple and hold-up calculations | calc | p.633 §9.5.2; p.634 §9.5.3A | high |
| AOE-2854 | magnetics | Transformer current ratings are rms secondary current; with a capacitor-input rectifier the rms current (and I^2R heating) greatly exceeds the dc output current, so choose an rms rating ~2x the dc load current (measured 3.95 Arms for a 2 A dc load); larger filter capacitors make it worse, full-wave rectification better. | I_rms,sec(rating) ~ 2 x I_dc | I_dc (A), rectifier type | Line-frequency transformer supplies | calc | p.633 §9.5.2, Fig. 9.51 | high |
| AOE-2855 | power | Do not trust a hand estimate of rectifier conduction angle (discharge by I = C dV/dt, then find when the rectified sine exceeds the capacitor voltage): winding resistance, leakage inductance and capacitor ESR widen it. Measure on the bench, or simulate in SPICE with measured transformer parameters. | measured conduction angle ~60 deg of each 180 deg half-cycle (Fig. 9.49 at 2 A) | R_wind, L_leak, ESR | Capacitor-input rectifiers | sim | p.633 §9.5.2; p.635 §9.5.4 | high |
| AOE-2856 | magnetics | Large transformer leakage inductance lengthens conduction angle losslessly but degrades load regulation, lags the input current (lower power factor), and with diode reverse recovery produces nasty voltage spikes. | qualitative | L_leak | Transformer selection | review | p.633 footnote 46 | high |
| AOE-2857 | power | Size the reservoir capacitor assuming a constant-current load equal to the average load current (exact for a linear-regulator load): C = I*dt/dV with dt = 8.33 ms (60 Hz full-wave); example 2 A and ~1 Vpp -> 16,700 uF. Rate its voltage for no load at high line (125-130 Vrms on 120 V mains) - 80 V chosen over 63 V for a nominal +/-50 V supply. | C = I_load*dt/V_ripple,pp; dt = 1/(2 f_line) | I_load (A), V_ripple (V), f_line (Hz) | Capacitor-input rectifiers | calc | p.633 §9.5.3A | high |
| AOE-2858 | components | Paralleling smaller reservoir capacitors (2 x 8200 uF, 35 x 50 mm, instead of one 16,000 uF/80 V, 40 x 80 mm) fits better and lowers the total series inductance. | C_total = sum(C_i); ESR, ESL divide | - | Filter capacitor banks | review | p.633-634 §9.5.3A | high |
| AOE-2859 | derating | Choose reservoir capacitors whose ripple-current rating comfortably exceeds the estimated rms capacitor current (~2x I_dc for capacitor-input rectifiers) and check ESR heating: example ~4 Arms estimated vs 2 x 5.8 Arms (85 C) rating; ESR 0.038 ohm max per cap -> pair <= 19 mohm -> ~0.15 W per capacitor. | I_ripple,rating > I_rms,est (~2 x I_dc); P_cap = I_rms^2*ESR | I_rms (A), ESR (ohm), rating @ temp | Filter capacitors | calc | p.634 §9.5.3A | high |
| AOE-2860 | power | Do not oversize filter capacitors: excess capacitance wastes space, shortens the conduction angle (raising rms/average current and transformer heating) and increases rectifier stress. | qualitative; minimum C that meets ripple spec | - | Capacitor-input rectifiers | review | p.634 §9.5.3A | high |
| AOE-2861 | protection | Put bleeder resistors across unregulated supply outputs: they provide a minimum load (unloaded output otherwise "soars", 54 V loaded -> 60 V unloaded in the example) and discharge the capacitors at power-off so no one damages circuits thinking no voltage is present. | R_bleed across each output | V_out, C | Unregulated supplies (Fig. 9.49 R1, R3) | inspect | p.634 §9.5.3A, §9.5.4 | high |
| AOE-2862 | components | Use power rectifiers, not signal diodes, in supplies: signal diodes (1N914/1N4148) are fast (ns), low-leakage (nA), low-capacitance (pF) but only ~100 mA and rarely > 100 V; rectifiers handle 1-25 A+ and 100-1000 V+ with uA-mA leakage and large capacitance. General-purpose rectifiers are fine at 60 Hz; switchers (20 kHz-1 MHz) need fast-recovery or Schottky rectifiers or synchronous MOSFETs. | signal diode: I <= ~100 mA, V_BR <= ~100 V; rectifier: 1-25 A, 100-1000 V | frequency, I, V | Rectifier selection | review | p.634 §9.5.3B | high |
| AOE-2863 | components | Rectifier examples: 1N4001-1N4007 (1 A) and 1N5400-1N5408 (3 A), 50-1000 V; 1N5817-1N5822 Schottky (1-3 A, 20-40 V, axial); MBR1545 (15 A) and 30CTQ045 (30 A) dual Schottky at 45 V (TO-220/D2PAK); MUR805-MUR1100 fast rectifiers (TO-220) to 1 kV; bridges from lead-mounted 1-6 A to heatsink-mounted 35 A or more. Higher-current rectifiers need heatsinking. A MOSFET bridge with an ideal-diode-bridge controller (LT4320) is an efficient option. | ratings as listed | I_avg, V_RRM | Part selection | review | p.634 §9.5.3B, footnote 48 | medium |
| AOE-2864 | emc | Always fit a series RC snubber across the transformer secondary of a rectifier supply: leakage inductance plus rectifier reverse recovery generate periodic microsecond-scale spikes of tens of volts that cause strong 120 Hz interference. The SPICE model of the Fig. 9.49 supply used Cs = 0.5 uF, Rs = 30 ohm. | RC snubber across secondary (example Cs = 0.5 uF, Rs = 30 ohm) | L_leak, diode t_rr | Line-frequency rectifier supplies | inspect | p.634 §9.5.3C, footnote 50 | high |
| AOE-2865 | power | Expect measured ripple below the full-half-period estimate (0.8 Vpp vs 1 Vpp estimate, because recharging starts after ~6 ms rather than ~8 ms) and loaded output above the rating-based estimate when lightly loaded or at high line (54 V at 2 A with line 3% high; 60 V unloaded). | ripple_measured < I*(1/(2f))/C | - | Validation of unregulated supply design | measure | p.635 §9.5.4, Fig. 9.51 | high |
| AOE-2866 | power | Capacitor-input rectification gives a poor power factor: the rms transformer current is about double the dc output current (3.95 Arms at 2 A dc) because current flows only in a short conduction angle; offline switchers can use power-factor correction to stay near unity. | PF = P_avg/(V_rms*I_rms); I_rms/I_dc ~ 2 | I_rms, I_dc | Line-powered supplies | measure | p.635 §9.5.4 | high |
| AOE-2867 | emc | Switching supplies are noisier than linear ones and the noise is measured differently at the load: into 50% load, a linear 5 V/0.3 A supply showed ~0.5 mVpp 120 Hz ripple; a 5 V/2.5 A switcher ~6 mVpp at its 50 kHz switching frequency across its output pins, but ~150 mV spikes (ringing ~15 MHz) at a load 50 cm away, induced by high-frequency ground currents. Measure switcher noise at the load, not only at the output pins. | ripple: linear ~0.5 mVpp; switcher ~6 mVpp at pins, ~150 mV spikes at load 50 cm away | measurement location, bandwidth | Supply noise verification (Fig. 9.53-9.54) | measure | p.636 §9.5.5, Fig. 9.53-9.54 | high |

### Auto-zero parameter ranges (p.339-340 §5.11.3)
Iq 15 uA-1.1 mA; Ib up to 0.5 nA max; Vos max 2-25 uV (typ to 0.15 uV); TCVos 5-20 nV/degC general (4-100 typ, to 250 max); en(1 kHz) 50-100 nV/sqrt(Hz); in 10-100x shot noise; SR 0.04-2.5 V/us; GBW 0.13-4.7 MHz; internal clock 1-50 kHz; overload recovery 4 us (OPA378) to 20 ms (LTC1150).

### Difference-amplifier formulas (p.348-356 §5.14)
- Gdiff = Rf/R1 (matched pairs); gain with source resistance: G_eff = G * R1/(R1 + Rs).
- Input impedance (Fig. 5.72): Zcm(each) = (G+1)*R1; Zcm(both tied) = (G+1)*R1/2; single-ended: Rin(-) = R1, Rin(+) = R1 + Rf.
- Common-mode range with internal divider: up to +/-Vs*(G+1)/G. AD8278 (R1 = 40k, Rf = 20k): G = 0.5: -3(Vs+0.1) to +3(Vs-1.5); G = 2: -1.5(Vs+0.1) to +1.5(Vs-1.5).
- CMRR-limited input error: V_err = Vcm / 10^(CMRR_dB/20). Examples: 200 V at 86 dB -> 10 mV; 160 V at 66 dB -> 80 mV.
- Resistor matching -> CMRR: 1% discrete resistors -> ~40 dB (book). Standard worst-case relation used in the checks below (not stated in this span): CMRR ~ (1 + G)/(4*eps), eps = resistor tolerance fraction.
- Johnson noise: en = 0.13*sqrt(R) nV/sqrt(Hz) (R in ohm); 100 ohm -> 1.3 nV/sqrt(Hz); 1 Mohm -> ~130 nV/sqrt(Hz).
- Current source: IL = Gdiff*(VA - VB)/Rs; op-amp current-source effective output capacitance C_eff = IL(max)/S.
- Precision resistor options (p.348): 0.1% ($0.10-0.20/100 pcs); Susumu RG 0.05%, 10 ppm/degC (~$1); Vishay MPM ratio tolerance to 0.01%, ratio tempco to 2 ppm/degC; LT5400 quad, ratio tempco 1 ppm/degC max.
- AD8278B worst case (p.349): gain +/-0.02% (G = 0.5 or 2), gain tempco 1 ppm/degC, Vos 100 uV, 1 uV/degC, CMRR 80 dB, ~$3.
- INA146 (p.355): G = 0.1 difference stage with filter node + external-gain second stage (overall 0.1-100); +/-100 V CM range at the expense of noise and offset.
- Difference amps in general (p.356): G < 10, CMRR < 85 dB min, 20-50 nV/sqrt(Hz), input resistance ~10k-100k.
- Table 5.7 (p.353, 27 parts: INA105, AMP03, INA134/154/132, AD8271/8273/8278, THAT1206, INA106/143, LT1991/1996/1995, INA146, INA117, AD629, INA149, AD8479, AD628, AD8275, AD830, AD8129/8130, EL5172, INA152, MAX4198): too OCR-garbled for reliable column alignment; only values confirmed in prose are used in rules.

### Noise formulas (p.324 §5.10.6; p.326 §5.10.8)
- Vn^2 = integral_{fa}^{fb} en(f)^2 df; Vn,rms = sqrt(Vn^2). White region: Vn = en * sqrt(BW).
- en-C noise current in a transimpedance amp: in = en * 2*pi*f*Cin (A/sqrt(Hz)), en in V/sqrt(Hz), Cin in F.
- Noise impedance Zn = en / in (ohm). LT1028: 850 ohm; LT1013: 315 kohm.
- Input current by capacitor ramp: Ib = C * dV/dt (365 pF, 0.20 mV/s -> 73 fA). Coax capacitance ~1 pF/cm (standard 50 ohm).

### Ch.5 §5.15-§5.17 + Review: instrumentation and fully differential amplifiers (pp. 356-390; lines 21123-23445)
### 2.1 Instrumentation-amplifier formulas (p.357-369)
| quantity | formula (ASCII) | symbols / units | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| 3-op-amp IA differential gain | Gdiff = 1 + 2*Rf/Rg ; Rg = 2*Rf/(G - 1) | Rf, Rg in ohm | p.357-358 §5.15.2, Fig. 5.77 |
| IA gain with mismatched front-end Rf | Gdiff = 1 + (Rf1 + Rf2)/Rg ; CM output shift (worst case) = (2*Rf/Rg)*dV | dV = half the differential input (V) | p.358 §5.15.3A |
| IA front-end headroom | Vcm +/- 0.5*Vdiff*(1 + 2*Rf/Rg) inside input-amp output swing | V | p.358 §5.15.3C |
| IA input-referred noise | en(RTI) = sqrt(en(in)^2 + (en(out)/G)^2 + 4*k*T*Rg) | en in V/rtHz; k = 1.38e-23 J/K; T in K; Rg in ohm | p.358 fn72 |
| Johnson noise shortcut | en = 0.13*sqrt(R) nV/rtHz | R in ohm, room temperature | p.358 fn72 (OCR dropped sqrt(R); value consistent with 4kTR) |
| IA CMRR vs gain | CMRR_total(dB) ~= 20*log10(Gdiff) + CMRR_outstage(dB) (e.g. 40 + 80 = 120 dB at G = 100) | dB | p.358 §5.15.2 |
| general INA principle (configs A, B, C, F) | Ig = dVin/Rg ; dVout(diff, config A) = (dVin/Rg)*(Rg + 2*Rf) | V, ohm | p.368-369 §5.16.10 |
| config A2 (Rg internal) | G = 1 + Rf/Ra + 2*Rf/Rg (as printed on Fig. 5.88; OCR) | ohm | p.368 Fig. 5.88 |
| config D (Fig. 5.89) | G = 1 + R2/R1 (as printed on Fig. 5.89; OCR) | ohm | p.369 Fig. 5.89 |
| required CMRR | CMRR_req(dB) = 20*log10(Vcm/V_err) (2.5 V vs 10 uV -> 108 dB) | V | p.364 §5.16.2 |
| REF-pin drive impedance | Z_src(REF) <= ~1e-6 * Rf | ohm | p.366 §5.16.5 |
| capacitive transient current | I = C*dV/dt (350 V/1 ns into 1 pF -> 350 mA; 5 V/ns into Coss -> <= 10 mA) | A, F, V/s | p.362-363 §5.15.5 |
| current-source error | I_err = Vos/R_sense ; DR = I_FS/I_err (25 uV/0.1 ohm = 0.25 mA of 5 A = 20,000:1) | A, V, ohm | p.367 §5.16.9 |

| LT6350 | A (low voltage) | 2.0 up | op-amp | 33 | gain error 0.6% |
| `ADA4941-1` | A (low voltage) | 2.0 up | op-amp | 30 | gain error 1% |
Table 5.10 notes (meaning): (a) fixed gain, PGA, or gain set by input resistor Rg; (b) RR output means Vout,diff = 2 x Vsupply max; (c) included in Vos spec; (d) includes feedback-resistor noise; (e) matched 2nd-order anti-alias filters, 7 and 10 MHz available; (g) 6600-x specifies 2.5 to 20 MHz 4th-order filters; (h) nominally Zin = Rg where G = Rf/Rg, but for "D" types it is greater than Rg; (i) filter, 6 to 27 MHz strappable; (j) -4 version compensated for G < 4; (k) settle to 1%; (m) most diff-to-diff amplifiers can convert SE input to diff output; (q) inst-amp input, diff-amp output; (r) G = 2; (s) R-2R ladder input, diff-amp output; (t) high CM Zout, like an isolation transformer; (v) inputs to -Vee; (w) RRO and inputs to -Vee or within 0.2 V of -Vee; (y) RRO and input beyond rails, to +/-15 V; (z) RTO. Comment-column recommended Rf values for fast parts cluster at 200-500 ohm (e.g. 200, 274, 349, 357, 402, 499 ohm); slower parts 1k-10k (row mapping garbled).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### 2.6 FDA terminated-input design equations (Fig. 5.104, p.383)
| step | equation | note |
|---|---|---|
| terminate | par(Rt, Rin) = Rs | Rin = input resistance of driven side (> RG1, since the + input is not a virtual ground) |
| Rin (driven side) | Rin = RG1 / (1 - RF/(2*(RG1 + RF))) | derived (the figure's Rin equation is lost in OCR); reproduces the book's example (Rt = 60.4 ohm) — conf medium |
| balance | RG2 = RG1 + par(Rs, Rt) | |
| gain | RF = G*RG2*(Rs + Rt)/(2*Rt) ; G = (Vout+ - Vout-)/Vin = (2*Rt/(Rt + Rs))*(RF/RG2) | G referred to loaded input Vin |
| example | Rs = 50, G = 2, RG1 = 200 -> Rt = 60.4, RG2 = 226, RF = 412 ohm (nearest 1%) | |
| unterminated (audio) | Rt omitted ; RG2 = RG1 + Rs ; G = RF/RG2 (w.r.t. open-circuit source) | |

### 2.7 Amplifier taxonomy (Review of Ch.5 ¶J-¶M, p.390; §5.15)
| type | in / out | Zin | gain | CMRR | noise / speed | other |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| difference amplifier | diff in, SE out | 25-100 kohm (tens of kohm) | 0.1-10 (typ 1-10) | 90-100 dB (¶J); typically < 90 dB (¶K); < 85 dB min (§5.15) | relatively high en (20-50 nV/rtHz, §5.15) | inputs can go beyond the rails (INA117: +/-200 V CM); G = Rf/R1 with REF grounded, SENSE to output; gain and CMRR degrade with non-zero source impedance |
| instrumentation amplifier | diff in, SE out | 10 Mohm-10 Gohm (¶J); 10 M-1 T (¶L) | 1-1000 via one resistor or pin-selected internal resistors | 110-140 dB at G = 100 (¶J); ~120 dB typ at high gain (¶L) | en < 10 nV/rtHz at high gain | BJT, JFET, MOSFET, auto-zero, PGA types; no CM beyond the rails (OCR reads "outputs") |
| fully differential amplifier | diff or SE in, diff out | ~Rf/G (config D) | per Rf/Rg or fixed | see Table 5.10 | up to 1 GHz or more, slew 1000 V/us or more, settling a few ns, 5 nV/rtHz or less | settable output CM (Vocm); for fast diff-input ADCs, balanced lines, RF |

### T6.2 VCVS (Sallen-Key, equal-component) lowpass design table (AoE Table 6.2, p.408)
Per section: R1 = R2 = R, C1 = C2 = C. Butterworth: R*C = 1/(2*pi*fc) for all sections. Bessel/Chebyshev: R*C = 1/(2*pi*cn*fc) per section. Highpass: swap R and C; Butterworth unchanged; Bessel/Chebyshev keep K, use 1/cn. Cascade sections in the listed order (lowest K first). conf = high (row tokens match standard AoE table; verified that products of K reproduce Fig. 6.29 gains).

| poles | section | Butterworth K | Bessel cn | Bessel K | Cheb 0.5 dB cn | Cheb 0.5 dB K | Cheb 2 dB cn | Cheb 2 dB K |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | 1 | 1.586 | 1.272 | 1.268 | 1.231 | 1.842 | 0.907 | 2.114 |
| 4 | 1 | 1.152 | 1.432 | 1.084 | 0.597 | 1.582 | 0.471 | 1.924 |
| 4 | 2 | 2.235 | 1.606 | 1.759 | 1.031 | 2.660 | 0.964 | 2.782 |
| 6 | 1 | 1.068 | 1.607 | 1.040 | 0.396 | 1.537 | 0.316 | 1.891 |
| 6 | 2 | 1.586 | 1.692 | 1.364 | 0.768 | 2.448 | 0.730 | 2.648 |
| 6 | 3 | 2.483 | 1.908 | 2.023 | 1.011 | 2.846 | 0.983 | 2.904 |
| 8 | 1 | 1.038 | 1.781 | 1.024 | 0.297 | 1.522 | 0.238 | 1.879 |
| 8 | 2 | 1.337 | 1.835 | 1.213 | 0.599 | 2.379 | 0.572 | 2.605 |
| 8 | 3 | 1.889 | 1.956 | 1.593 | 0.861 | 2.711 | 0.842 | 2.821 |
| 8 | 4 | 2.610 | 2.192 | 2.184 | 1.006 | 2.913 | 0.990 | 2.946 |

Section Q from K (equal-component): Q = 1/(3 - K) (Eq. 6.7). E.g. 8-pole Chebyshev 2 dB last section K = 2.946 -> Q = 18.5 (derived) -> by fT >= 50*Q^2*fc needs fT >= ~17,000 x fc (derived, illustrates why high-ripple, high-order VCVS sections stress the op-amp).

### Key equations
- Butterworth: |H| = 1/sqrt(1 + (f/fc)^(2n)) (Eq. 6.3). Chebyshev: |H| = 1/sqrt(1 + eps^2*Cn^2(f/fc)) (Eq. 6.4).
- Group delay: tau_g = -d(phi)/d(omega).
- Gyrator: Z_in = R^2/Z, L = C*R^2. NIC: Z_in = -Z. GIC: Z_in = Z5*Z1*Z3/(Z2*Z4), L = (R1*R3*R5/R2)*C.
- Sallen-Key general: fc = 1/(2*pi*tau*sqrt(m*n)), Q = sqrt(m*n)/(1 + m + m*n*(1-K)), m = R1/R2, n = C1/C2, tau = R2*C2. Equal parts: fc = 1/(2*pi*R*C), Q = 1/(3-K).
- Unity-gain SK for target Q: a = n/(2*Q^2) - 1, m = a + sqrt(a^2 - 1).
- State-variable BP (IC): R_F = 5.03e7/f0, R_Q = 1e5/(3.48*Q + G - 1), R_G = 3.16e4*Q/G (ohm).
- 4-op-amp state-variable BP: f0 = 1/(2*pi*R_F*C), Q = R1/R_Q, G = R1/R_G.
- Biquad: f0 = 1/(2*pi*R_F*C), BW = 1/(2*pi*R_B*C), G = R_B/R_G, Q = R_B/R_F.
- Twin-T: f_notch = 1/(2*pi*R*C). Bridged differentiator: f_notch = 1/(2*pi*C*sqrt(3*R1*R2)).
- First-order allpass: delta_phi = -2*arctan(omega*R*C).
- Switched-capacitor: R_eq = 1/(f_clk*C1); f_c = f_clk/50 or f_clk/100.
- Sampling: f_samp >= 2*f_sig(max); dynamic range = 6n dB for n bits. One-pole IIR: b = exp(-t_s/RC).
- FIR windowed sinc: a_k = sin(2*pi*k*f_n)/(2*pi*k*f_n), f_n = fc/fs; half-band (f_n = 0.25): a_k = sin(pi*k/2)/(pi*k/2); Hamming w(k) = 0.54 + 0.46*cos(2*pi*k/N); normalize sum(a_k) = 1.

### RC-cascade shrinkage factor (derived from AOE-2239, computed)
| n buffered identical RC sections | f3dB(n)/f3dB(1) |
|---|---|

| 2 | 0.6436 |

|---|---|---|---|
| Hamming (raised cosine) | [0.54, 0.46, 0, 0] | ~55 dB (also quoted ~60 dB) | steepest of the three |
| Blackman-Harris 3-term | [0.42323, 0.49755, 0.07922, 0] | ~85 dB | softer |
| Blackman-Harris 4-term | [0.35875, 0.48829, 0.14128, 0.01168] | ~105 dB | softest |

| Series vs antiresonant spacing | within ~0.1% (fa ~0.1% above fs) |
| Typical 1 MHz crystal model | C0 = 4 pF; L1 = 1 H; C1 = 0.02 pF; R1 ~75 ohm (OCR "750"); Q = 1e5 |
| Parallel-mode load capacitance CL | typically 10-35 pF |
| Measured CTS MP100 (10.0 MHz +/-45 ppm, series mode) | fs = 10.000086 MHz (+8.6 ppm); R1 = 4.736 ohm (spec max 50 ohm); C0 = 5.5 pF; fa = 10.02245 MHz; fp = 10.00355 MHz at CL = 30 pF; L1 = 10.3324 mH; C1 = 0.024515 pF; parallel-mode equivalent part MP101 |
| Pulling by CL = 30 pF | ~2000 ppm (parallel); < 1 ppm (series) |
| Phase slope near resonance | ~13 deg/ppm (R1 = 4.7 ohm); ~1.3 deg/ppm (R1 = 50 ohm) |
| Fundamental-mode range | ~10 kHz to ~30 MHz; overtone to ~250 MHz |
| Easy stock frequencies | 100 kHz, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 5.0, 10.0 MHz; 3.579545 MHz (TV color burst); 32.768 kHz (/2^15 = 1 Hz) |
| Crystal cost | much less than a dollar |
| VCXO deviation | +/-10 to +/-100 ppm typical; wide units to +/-1000 ppm |
| Inverter oscillator series R2 | ~330 kohm at 32 kHz; ~1 kohm at 5 MHz |
| Fig 7.38 example load caps | 27 pF pair (74LVC1404, Fig 7.38D); 32 pF trim caps (Fig 7.38D, E); 18 pF pair on 10.0 MHz microcontroller crystal (Fig 7.38F) |
| MEMS oscillators | less stable than quartz; SiTime parts as small as 2 mm x 2.5 mm x 0.8 mm |
Formulas: fp = fs*(1 + C1/(2*(C0 + CL))); CL = fs*C1/(2*(fp - fs)) - C0; C1 = 2*(1 - fp/fa)/(1/C0 - 1/(C0 + CL)); L1 = 1/(C1*(2*pi*fs)^2). (p.445 fn.40-41)

### Four-step procedure: noise-figure contours -> en, in (p.489-491 §8.4)
| step | action | formula |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | read NF vs Ic for each Rs along horizontal lines of the contour plot (half-decade Ic steps) | - |
| 2 | read NF vs Rs for each Ic along vertical lines -> approximate optimum Ic for a given Rs | - |
| 3 | invert NF definition at small Rs to get en (large Rs to get in) | en = sqrt(4kT*Rs)*sqrt(10^(NF/10) - 1) |
| 4 | find 1/f corner from the two spot frequencies, then the spectrum | fc = fL*((enL/enH)^2 - 1); en(f) = enH*sqrt(1 + fc/f) |

### Key noise formulas in this span
| quantity | formula | symbols / units | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| total input noise density | vn^2 = 4*k*T*Rsig + en^2 + (in*Rsig)^2 (V^2/Hz) | k = 1.38e-23 J/K; T (K); Rsig signal source resistance (ohm); en (V/rtHz); in (A/rtHz) | p.533 eq. 8.42 |
| noise resistance | Rn = en/in (ohm) | source resistance for minimum NF | p.529 §8.9.1H |
| en-dominant condition | Rs_seen <= Rn/5 ... Rn/30 | Rs_seen = impedance at op-amp inputs | p.529 §8.9.1H |
| shot-noise current | in = sqrt(2*q*Ib) (A/rtHz) | q = 1.602e-19 C; invalid for bias-cancelled inputs (actual 10x-40x higher) | p.527 §8.9.1F |
| capacitive noise current | in_C = en*omega*Cin = en*2*pi*f*Cin (A/rtHz) | Cin (F) incl. external capacitance | p.533 fn 81, §8.11.3 |
| integrated noise with 1/f | vn^2 = enH^2*[(f2 - f1) + fc*ln(f2/f1)] (V^2 rms) | enH white noise density; f1, f2 band edges; fc 1/f corner (Hz) | eq. 8.59 (§8.13.3, p.564) referenced p.531 |
| white-noise integrated | Vn = en*sqrt(BW) | BW (Hz) | p.531 §8.9.1L |
| 0.1-10 Hz p-p from rms | Vn(p-p, 0.1-10 Hz) ~ 6 * Vn(rms, brick-wall 0.1-10 Hz) | empirical over a few hundred op-amps | p.532 §8.9.1M |
| differential-pair noise | en_pair = sqrt(2)*en_single | | p.535 §8.9.5B |
| paralleled devices | en_N = en_1/sqrt(N) | N identical paralleled input devices | p.537 §8.10.1 |
| transformer step-up | V_amp = n*V_s; Z_seen = n^2*Zs | n:1 turns ratio | p.535 §8.10, (a) p.536 |
| resistor Johnson noise | 50 ohm -> 0.9 nV/rtHz | at room temperature | p.535 §8.10 |

| item | value | source |
|---|---|---|
| ZTX951 (pnp) rbb' | ~1.2 ohm, en 0.17 nV/rtHz at Ic = 10 mA | p.535 §8.9.5B |
| ZTX851 (npn) rbb' | ~1.4 ohm | p.535 §8.9.5B |
| ZTX951 vs ZTX851 Early voltage | VA = 120 V vs 410 V (Table 8.1a) | p.535 fn 85 |
| LSK389B | en 0.9 nV/rtHz typ, 1.9 max at ID = 2 mA; measured 1.34 (1.9 for the pair); Ciss = 25 pF; gm >= 20 mS at 3 mA; worst-case offset 20 mV | p.534-535 §8.9.5 fn 86 |
| hybrid front-end voltage gain | ~x120 (BJT), ~x12 (JFET) | p.535 §8.9.5A |

| Jensen JT-115K-E | 1:10 (150 ohm:15 kohm); +/-0.15 dB 20 Hz-20 kHz; -3 dB at 2.5 Hz and 90 kHz; THD < 0.1% at 20 Hz, 0.001% at 1 kHz; CMR 110 dB at 60 Hz; NF ~0.4 dB amplifier, ~1.5 dB overall | p.535-536 §8.10 |
| Signal Recovery Model 1900 | 1:100 and 1:1000; winding resistance 0.04 ohm; min NF ~0.5 dB for Rs = 0.8-10 ohm | p.536 §8.10 |
| SR560 | lowest NF at source impedance ~500 kohm; 100 ohm source direct gives NF = 12 dB | p.535 §8.10 |
| transformer-feedback amp (Fig. 8.68) | 650 pV/rtHz; BF862 at 10 mA (0.9 nV/rtHz each, pair 0.65); gain x10 by turns ratio; band ~10 kHz-75 MHz; 6.5 mm OD high-mu tape-wound toroid; 5 GHz transistors; inverted-cascode re = 12 ohm | p.536-537 §8.10.1 |
| SQUID | can measure voltages of 1e-15 V | p.536 §8.10(b) |

Other Table 8.3 facts: over 60 of the listed op-amps come in SOT-23, prices as low as $0.72 (qty 25) (p.521 footnote 71). CFB parts (THS3xxx, AD844, AD8001, OPA69x, AD8009, LMH6702) show in of 2-46 pA/rtHz, i.e. far higher current noise than VFB bipolar parts of similar en.

| 1e-9 | 5 | 4k | 15 | 150 |

### Equivalent noise bandwidth (ENBW) factors (pp.561-564 §8.13.1-8.13.2; Table 8.4 with f1 = 0)
| band-limiting filter | ENBW B |
|---|---|
| 1-pole RC lowpass (= 1-pole Butterworth) | 1.57*f3dB (pi/2) |
| 2 cascaded buffered RC lowpass sections | 1.22*f3dB |
| 2-pole Butterworth lowpass | 1.11*f3dB |
| 3-pole Butterworth lowpass | 1.05*f3dB |
| 4-pole Butterworth lowpass | 1.025*f3dB |
| m-pole Butterworth lowpass | ((pi/2m)/sin(pi/2m))*f3dB |
| parallel RLC (current in, voltage out) | 1/(4RC) = pi*f0/(2Q), Q = 2*pi*f0*R*C |
| series RLC (voltage in, current out) | R/(4L) = pi*f0/(2Q), Q = 2*pi*f0*L/R |
| averaging a dc signal for time T | 1/(2T) |
| rectangular window / lock-in over time T | 1/T |

### Table 8.4 noise integrals: mean-square noise v_n^2 in band f1..f2 (p.564; en2 = density at f2)
OCR of Table 8.4 is garbled; entries below were re-derived by integration and agree with the legible fragments and with the lowpass ENBW factors above (conf medium).
| filter | white (en const) | pink (en^2 prop. 1/f) | red (en^2 prop. 1/f^2) |
|---|---|---|---|
| brick-wall | en^2*(f2 - f1) | en2^2*f2*ln(f2/f1) | en2^2*f2*(f2 - f1)/f1 |
| 1-pole (RC highpass f1 + RC lowpass f2) | en^2*(pi/2)*f2^2/(f1 + f2) | en2^2*f2^3*ln(f2/f1)/(f2^2 - f1^2) | en2^2*pi*f2^3/(2*f1*(f1 + f2)) |
| 2-pole Butterworth | en^2*pi*f2^4/(2*sqrt(2)*(f1 + f2)*(f1^2 + f2^2)) | en2^2*f2^5*ln(f2/f1)/(f2^4 - f1^4) | en2^2*pi*f2^5/(2*sqrt(2)*f1*(f1 + f2)*(f1^2 + f2^2)) |
| m-pole Butterworth | en^2*((pi/2m)/sin(pi/2m))*(f2 - f1)/(1 - (f1/f2)^(2m)) | en2^2*f2*ln(f2/f1)/(1 - (f1/f2)^(2m)) | en2^2*((pi/2m)/sin(pi/2m))*(f2/f1)*(f2 - f1)/(1 - (f1/f2)^(2m)) |
rms noise = sqrt(v_n^2). Setting f1 = 0 is valid for white noise only. Eq. (8.58) (asymmetric 1-pole highpass + 2-pole Butterworth lowpass, pink/red) is too garbled to transcribe.

### 1/f noise bookkeeping (pp.564-567)
- Brick-wall straddling the corner: vn^2 = enH^2*(f2 - f1 + fc*ln(f2/f1)) (eq. 8.59).
- Corner from two points: fc = fL*((enL/enH)^2 - 1) (eq. 8.60).
- Datasheet p-p to rms: Vn(rms) ~ Vn(pp)/6.
- Ratio of pure-1/f power, band fa..10 Hz vs 0.1..10 Hz: fa = 1 uHz -> 3.5; fa = 1e-12 Hz -> 6.5.

### TIA calculated fc and fx, Cexternal = 10 pF, Rf = 1 Mohm (p.540 Fig. 8.72B; fc = sqrt(fRC*fT), fx per eq. 8.45)
| op-amp | en (nV/rtHz) | Camp (pF) | Cin total (pF) | fT (MHz) | fc (kHz) | fx (kHz) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LF411 | 25 | 2 | 12 | 4 | 230 | 67 |
| OPA627 | 4.5 | 15 | 25 | 16 | 320 | 178 |
| OPA637 | 4.5 | 15 | 25 | 80 | 715 | 178 |
| OPA656 | 7 | 3.5 | 13.5 | 230 | 1650 | 212 |
| OPA657 | 4.8 | 5.2 | 15.2 | 1600 | 4100 | 274 |
(values cross-checked against eqs. 8.43-8.46: e.g. LF411 fRC = 1/(2*pi*1e6*12e-12) = 13.3 kHz, fc = sqrt(13.3e3*4e6) = 230 kHz, fx = 2e7/(25*12) = 67 kHz.)

### TIA noise-current terms (input-referred, A/rtHz) (pp.538-539 §8.11.2-8.11.4)
- Rf Johnson: sqrt(4kT/Rf); 1 Mohm, 25C -> 0.128 pA/rtHz (0.13 pA/rtHz, p.540).
- Amplifier voltage noise through Rf: en/par(Rf,Rs).
- Amplifier current noise: in (direct).
- enC: 2*pi*en*Cin*f, flattened above fc = 1/(2*pi*Rf*Cf), falling -6 dB/oct above fc when fc = fGM.
- Signal shot noise: sqrt(2*q*Iin); equals Rf Johnson current when Iin*Rf = 2kT/q = 50 mV.
- Total: root-sum-square of the above.

### Environments to avoid for sensitive instruments (p.581 §8.16.1A)
Near radio/TV stations (RF), subways (impulsive, powerline garbage), high-voltage lines (radio interference, frying sounds), motors and elevators (powerline spikes), buildings with triac dimmers/heater controllers (spikes), large transformers (magnetic pickup), arc welders (all sorts). Raising signal levels improves signal-to-interference ratio.

### Review of Chapter 8 rules of thumb not given ids above (pp.590-593; primary sections mined by other forks)
- Independent noise sources add as root-sum-square; white noise vn = en*sqrt(B); op-amp input noise vn^2 = 4kTRs + en^2 + (in*Rs)^2 (Fig. 8.58).
- Noise amplitude is Gaussian except burst (popcorn/telegraph) noise, which can masquerade as steeper/flatter-than-pink low-frequency spectra (§8.13.4B).
- Excess (current-induced, ~1/f) resistor noise: worst in carbon composition, insignificant in wirewound.
- BJT: en^2 = 4kT(re/2 + rbb'); en falls with Ic (limit rbb'); in = sqrt(2*q*IB) rises with Ic; noise resistance Rn = en/in; NF minimum at Rs = Rn.
- JFET: en^2 ~ 4kT*(2/3)/gm (Johnson noise of ~(2/3)/gm); en falls only as ID^-0.25; in = sqrt(2*q*IG); watch 5:1 IDSS/VGS(th) spreads, gate current rising with temperature and drain voltage, poor LF noise.
- NF = 10*log10(1 + vn^2/(4kTRs)); do not add series resistance to a low-Rs source to improve NF - choose a better-matched amplifier or a signal transformer.
- Op-amps: FET-input for high source impedance (lowest in), BJT-input for low source impedance (lowest en); CMOS lower in but higher en than JFET; auto-zero parts have no 1/f tail but higher broadband noise, spectral peaks and chopping artifacts; first filter candidates by accuracy, speed, power, supply and swing, then choose by noise.
- Capacitance multiplier outboard of any supply can cut noise by 40 dB or more (Fig. 8.122).
- Lock-in: modulate at a few hundred Hz (above the 1/f corner), amplify, demodulate, lowpass to the original signal bandwidth.

### LM317 application-hint formulas (p.604-607 §9.3.4, Fig. 9.14)
| Item | Formula / value |
|---|---|
| Minimum-load divider | R1 = 1.25 V / I_out(min); R1 <= 125 ohm for 10 mA |
| Output with ADJ current (eq. 9.1) | V_out = V_ref*(1 + R2/R1) + I_adj*R2 |
| Worst-case ADJ error | I_adj = 100 uA, R1 = 125 ohm -> ~1% (on top of Vref 1% or 4%) |
| Soft-start slew | S = dV/dt = I/C; I = Vref/R1 -> 1 V/ms (R1 = 124 ohm, C = 10 uF); I = Vbe/R3 -> 0.01 V/ms (R3 = 6.8 k) |
| Soft-start start levels | G: jumps to Vref (1.25 V); H: jumps to Vref + Vbe (~1.8 V) |
| Protection diodes | required for V_o > 25 V and large C1, C2 (Fig. 9.14F) |
| Bandgap internals | dVbe = (kT/q) ln 10 ~ 60 mV; 25 uA per side, 50 uA total; Vbe ~600 mV at -2.1 mV/C; V_bg ~1.2 V |

### Table 9.3 — Low-dropout linear regulators (p.614-615): body not transcribed
The OCR scatters Table 9.3's ~110 rows (columns: packages, Vin min-max, Vout, Iout max, Vdo, accuracy, Iq, noise Vn@Vout, line reg, Cout min, ESR min/max, price) into disconnected number runs that cannot be re-aligned to part numbers reliably, so no row values are given here. Notes that are legible: (a) LDOs typically drop < 100 mV except at the highest currents, ADJ versions of some parts are less accurate than laser-trimmed fixed versions; (b) noise 10 Hz-100 kHz without NR cap unless noted; (e1) 100 Hz-100 kHz or 200 Hz-100 kHz; (e2) 10 Hz-10 kHz; (e3) 300 Hz-50 kHz; (e4) 10 Hz-1 MHz; (f) with NR cap; (f2) if ADJ bypassed, larger output caps required; (h) minimum load current; (o) load-dump rated: withstands input transients (or continuous inputs) to 60 V (40 V some parts) without passing the spike; (x) reversed input with excess current may damage part - Schottky recommended; triangle = input-output back diode conducts (limit current, reverse supply not allowed); square = protected against reverse-polarity input; bullet (reverse block) = blocks current when Vin < Vout. Comment A (LT3080-type): current-programmed; control dropout 1.6 V max; 1 mA min load.

| Measured ripple | ~0.8 Vpp (vs 1 Vpp estimate) |
| Measured dc out | 54 V at 2 A (line +3%); 60 V unloaded |
| Measured conduction angle | ~60 deg per 180 deg half-cycle |
| Measured secondary current | 3.95 Arms at 2 A dc |
| SPICE model (fn 50) | primary R = 0.467 ohm, primary leakage L = 1.63 (OCR "I.6 3 jiH", read as 1.63 uH, uncertain), magnetizing L_M = 80 mH, turns ratio 0.365, secondary R = 0.217 ohm, secondary leakage 20 uH, snubber Cs = 0.5 uF / Rs = 30 ohm, C = 14,000 uF with ESR 0.01 ohm, loads 27 ohm each side |

### Rectifier examples (§9.5.3B)
| Part(s) | Rating |
|---|---|
| 1N914 / 1N4148 (signal) | ns speed, nA leakage, pF; <= ~100 mA; V_BR rarely > 100 V |
| 1N4001-1N4007 | 1 A, 50-1000 V |
| 1N5400-1N5408 | 3 A, 50-1000 V |
| 1N5817-1N5822 (Schottky, axial) | 1-3 A, 20-40 V |
| MBR1545 / 30CTQ045 (dual Schottky, TO-220/D2PAK) | 15 A / 30 A at 45 V |
| MUR805-MUR1100 (TO-220) | fast rectifiers to 1 kV (current rating OCR-garbled "6 A") |
| Plastic bridges | 1-6 A lead-mounted; to 35 A+ heatsink-mounted |

### Linear vs switcher output noise (Fig. 9.53, 50% rated load)
| Supply | Measured |
|---|---|
| Linear 5 V, 0.3 A | ~0.5 mVpp at 120 Hz |
| Switcher 5 V, 2.5 A, at output pins | ~6 mVpp at 50 kHz (dithered) |
| Same switcher at load 50 cm away | ~150 mV spikes, ringing ~15 MHz |

## 3. Mechanizable checks
### Ch.5 §5.10-§5.14: choosing precision op-amps, auto-zero amplifiers, DMM front ends, difference amplifiers (pp. 319-356; lines 18822-21122)
- `CHECK-opamp-cm-headroom`: inputs (V+ V, V- V, Vin_min V, Vin_max V, Vout_min V, Vout_max V, in_headroom_neg/pos V, out_headroom_neg/pos V from datasheet) -> Vin_min - V- >= in_headroom_neg AND V+ - Vin_max >= in_headroom_pos AND same for output -> pass if all four hold -> margin = min of the four differences (V) -> source rows (AOE-2008, AOE-2009).
- `CHECK-opamp-offset-fullscale`: inputs (Vos_max uV, TCVos_max uV/degC, dT degC, gain G, V_FS V, error_budget ppm) -> err_ppm = 1e6 * (Vos_max + TCVos_max*dT) * 1e-6 * G / V_FS -> pass if err_ppm <= error_budget -> margin = error_budget - err_ppm (ppm) -> source rows (AOE-2007, AOE-2012, AOE-2016).
- `CHECK-opamp-noise-impedance`: inputs (en V/sqrt(Hz), in A/sqrt(Hz), Zs ohm) -> Zn = en/in; e_i = in*Zs; e_tot = sqrt(en^2 + e_i^2 + 4kT*Zs) -> pass if Zs <= Zn/3 (current noise < ~1/3 of en; threshold is a heuristic for "Zs << Zn") -> margin = Zn/Zs -> source rows (AOE-2031, AOE-2032).
- `CHECK-opamp-integrated-noise`: inputs (en_w V/sqrt(Hz), fc Hz, fa Hz, fb Hz) -> Vn = en_w*sqrt(fc*ln(fb/fa) + (fb - fa)) (1/f + white integral of en^2 = en_w^2*(1 + fc/f)) -> pass if Vn <= Vn_budget -> margin = Vn_budget/Vn -> source rows (AOE-2018, AOE-2019, AOE-2021).
- `CHECK-opamp-ib-max-not-typ`: inputs (Ib_used_in_budget A, Ib_max_datasheet A at T_max) -> pass if Ib_used >= Ib_max(T_max) -> margin = Ib_used/Ib_max -> source rows (AOE-2024, AOE-2025, AOE-2012).
- `CHECK-opamp-current-noise-bias-cancel`: inputs (in_datasheet A/sqrt(Hz), Ib_net A, I_B_uncancelled A or null, bias_cancel bool) -> in_min = sqrt(2*q*Ib_net); if bias_cancel: in_est = sqrt(2)*sqrt(2*q*I_B_uncancelled) -> flag if in_datasheet < in_min, or (bias_cancel and in_datasheet < 0.5*in_est) -> pass if no flag -> margin = in_datasheet / max(in_min, in_est) -> source rows (AOE-2034, AOE-2035, AOE-2036).
- `CHECK-opamp-cmrr-error`: inputs (dVcm V at f, CMRR_min_dB(f), Vos_max V, error_budget V) -> V_cm_err = dVcm / 10^(CMRR_min_dB/20) -> pass if V_cm_err + Vos_max <= error_budget -> margin = error_budget - (V_cm_err + Vos_max) -> source rows (AOE-2039, AOE-2115, AOE-2099).
- `CHECK-opamp-psrr-ripple`: inputs per rail (ripple_pk V at f, PSRR_dB(f) per rail, budget V) -> V_ps = sum over rails of ripple/10^(PSRR_dB/20) -> pass if V_ps <= budget -> margin = budget - V_ps; flag if any rail's PSRR(f) spec missing -> source rows (AOE-2041).
- `CHECK-opamp-slew-fpbw`: inputs (S V/us, Vpp V, f_max Hz, fT MHz, m) -> FPBW = S*1e6/(pi*Vpp); S_est = 0.32*m*fT -> pass if FPBW >= f_max -> margin = FPBW/f_max -> source rows (AOE-2042, AOE-2043).
- `CHECK-opamp-slew-settling`: inputs (dV V, S V/us, t_budget us, ts_datasheet us) -> t_slew = dV/S -> pass if t_slew + ts_small_signal <= t_budget -> margin = t_budget - t_total -> source rows (AOE-2044, AOE-2081).
- `CHECK-opamp-decomp-min-gain`: inputs (noise_gain, G_min_stable) -> pass if noise_gain >= G_min_stable -> margin = noise_gain/G_min_stable -> source rows (AOE-2045).
- `CHECK-capload-drive-current`: inputs (C_load F, slew_req V/s, I_out_max A) -> I_req = C_load*slew_req -> pass if I_req <= I_out_max (and inside driver SOA) -> margin = I_out_max/I_req -> source rows (AOE-2053, AOE-2056).
- `CHECK-dac-buffer-lsb`: inputs (V_FS V, N bits, Rout_DAC ohm, Ib_max A, Vos_max V) -> V_LSB = V_FS/2^N; pass if Ib_max*Rout_DAC < V_LSB and Vos_max < V_LSB -> margin = V_LSB/max(Ib_max*Rout_DAC, Vos_max) -> source rows (AOE-2054).
- `CHECK-az-overload-recovery`: inputs (can_saturate bool, tr_datasheet us, t_allowed us, clamp_present bool) -> pass if not can_saturate or clamp_present or tr <= t_allowed -> margin = t_allowed/tr -> source rows (AOE-2068).
- `CHECK-az-spike-source-impedance`: inputs (i_spike_pp A (measured or assumed nA-scale), Rs ohm, gain G, output_filter_fc Hz, budget V) -> v_in_spike = i_spike_pp*Rs; flag if v_in_spike > budget and no output RC below a few hundred Hz -> source rows (AOE-2063, AOE-2064, AOE-2066).
- `CHECK-thermal-emf-budget`: inputs per junction pair (S_uV_per_C from §2 table, dT_C across the pair) -> V_th = sum(S*dT) -> pass if V_th <= offset_budget -> margin = budget - V_th -> source rows (AOE-2076, AOE-2077).
- `CHECK-diffamp-cmrr-from-resistors`: inputs (G, eps resistor tolerance fraction or ratio match, CMRR_required dB) -> CMRR_wc = 20*log10((1+G)/(4*eps)) (standard worst case; book anchor: 1% -> ~40 dB) -> pass if CMRR_wc >= CMRR_required -> margin = CMRR_wc - CMRR_required (dB) -> source rows (AOE-2102, AOE-2099).
- `CHECK-diffamp-source-impedance`: inputs (Rs ohm, R1 ohm, gain_error_budget fraction, integrated bool) -> gain_err = Rs/(R1 + Rs) -> pass if gain_err <= budget; flag CMRR invalid if integrated and Rs > 0 (spec at Rs = 0) -> source rows (AOE-2110, AOE-2111).
- `CHECK-diffamp-cm-range`: inputs (Vs V, G, Vcm_max_signal V, datasheet CM limits V) -> limit = min(Vs*(G+1)/G, datasheet) -> pass if abs(Vcm_max_signal) <= limit -> margin = limit - abs(Vcm) -> source rows (AOE-2114).
- `CHECK-shunt-kelvin-error`: inputs (I_max A, Rs ohm, R_conn ohm, kelvin bool, P_rating W, error_budget) -> P = I_max^2*Rs; err = 0 if kelvin else R_conn/Rs -> pass if P <= P_rating and err <= budget -> source rows (AOE-2103).
- `CHECK-current-source-ranges`: inputs (IL_max A, IL_min A, Rs ohm, Vos_max V, Ib_max A, compliance V) -> pass if Rs*IL_min >= 100e-6 and Vos_max <= 0.1*Rs*IL_min (heuristic for "small compared with") and Ib_max <= 0.01*IL_min (heuristic) and Rs*IL_max leaves compliance -> margin = min of ratios -> source rows (AOE-2105).
- `CHECK-ground-return-balance`: inputs (I_return A, R_gnd ohm, mismatch fraction, budget V) -> V_err = I_return*R_gnd*mismatch (mismatch = 1 if no balancing current) -> pass if V_err <= budget -> source rows (AOE-2092).

### Ch.5 §5.15-§5.17 + Review: instrumentation and fully differential amplifiers (pp. 356-390; lines 21123-23445)
- `CHECK-inamp-input-stage-headroom`: inputs Vcm (V), Vdiff_max (V), Rf (ohm), Rg (ohm), V+ / V- (V), front-end output swing limits Vsw+ / Vsw- (V from each rail) -> Vhi = Vcm + 0.5*Vdiff*(1 + 2*Rf/Rg), Vlo = Vcm - 0.5*Vdiff*(1 + 2*Rf/Rg) -> pass if Vhi <= V+ - Vsw+ and Vlo >= V- + Vsw- at every gain setting and Vcm extreme -> margin = min(V+ - Vsw+ - Vhi, Vlo - V- - Vsw-) (V) -> source rows AOE-2131, AOE-2165.
- `CHECK-inamp-gain-resistor`: inputs G_target, Rf (ohm; datasheet internal value, e.g. 3 kohm INA103), Rg_chosen (ohm, E96), gain tolerance (%) -> Rg_ideal = 2*Rf/(G - 1); G_actual = 1 + 2*Rf/Rg_chosen -> pass if abs(G_actual - G_target)/G_target <= tolerance (after in-system calibration if used) -> margin = tolerance - error (%) -> AOE-2125, AOE-2136.
- `CHECK-inamp-cmrr-required`: inputs Vcm (V), FS (V), resolution (fraction of FS), f_cm (Hz), CMRR_min(f_cm) (dB, datasheet curve at the chosen gain and source imbalance) -> CMRR_req = 20*log10(Vcm/(resolution*FS)) -> pass if CMRR_min(f_cm) >= CMRR_req -> margin = CMRR_min - CMRR_req (dB) -> AOE-2152, AOE-2153, AOE-2158, AOE-2126.
- `CHECK-inamp-noise-rti`: inputs en_in, en_out (V/rtHz), G, Rg (ohm), T (K), BW (Hz), budget (V rms) -> en_RTI = sqrt(en_in^2 + (en_out/G)^2 + 4*k*T*Rg); Vn = en_RTI*sqrt(BW) -> pass if Vn <= budget -> margin = budget - Vn -> AOE-2127.
- `CHECK-inamp-ref-drive-impedance`: inputs Rf of output stage (ohm), Z_src at REF (ohm) -> limit = 1e-6*Rf -> pass if Z_src <= limit (e.g. few milliohm: needs an op-amp buffer) -> margin = limit/Z_src -> AOE-2161.
- `CHECK-protect-series-limiter-dissipation`: inputs Vin_max (V), I_limit_max (A; saturation current incl. R4), package P_max (W) -> P = Vin_max*I_limit_max -> pass if P <= P_max -> margin = P_max - P -> AOE-2139, AOE-2140.
- `CHECK-protect-capacitive-transient`: inputs step dV (V), rise time tr (s), series capacitance C (F; Coss/Ciss or parasitic), series resistance R (ohm), op-amp/clamp current limit I_max (A), Vgs_max (V) -> I = C*dV/tr; V_R = I*R -> pass if I <= I_max and V_R < Vgs_max -> margin = min(I_max - I, Vgs_max - V_R) -> AOE-2146, AOE-2147, AOE-2149.
- `CHECK-isense-dynamic-range`: inputs Vos_max (V), R_sense (ohm), I_FS (A), DR_required, P_rating (W) -> I_err = Vos/R_sense; DR = I_FS/I_err; P = I_FS^2*R_sense -> pass if DR >= DR_required and P <= P_rating -> margins: DR/DR_required, P_rating - P -> AOE-2168, AOE-2169, AOE-2155.
- `CHECK-front-end-rc-rolloff`: inputs R (ohm), C (F), spec BW (Hz) -> f3dB = 1/(2*pi*R*C) -> pass if f3dB >= k*spec BW (book example k = 2.5: 500 kHz for 200 kHz) -> margin = f3dB/spec BW -> AOE-2137.
- `CHECK-fda-input-cm-range`: inputs Vocm (V), Rf, Rg (ohm), source type (balanced: Vin(CM); single-ended: Vin over signal extremes), Vicm_min, Vicm_max (V; datasheet, usually >= V- + 1 V unless NRI, NRI floor -Vee - 0.2 V) -> G = Rf/Rg; Vpin = (Vocm + G*Vin(CM))/(1 + G) with Vin(CM) = Vin/2 for single-ended, evaluated at min and max Vin -> pass if Vicm_min <= Vpin <= Vicm_max for all extremes -> margin = min distance (V) -> AOE-2197, AOE-2206, AOE-2217.
- `CHECK-fda-termination`: inputs Rs (ohm), G, RG1, chosen Rt, RG2, RF (ohm) -> Rin = RG1/(1 - RF/(2*(RG1 + RF))); Zin_seen = par(Rt, Rin); RG2_ideal = RG1 + par(Rs, Rt); G_actual = (2*Rt/(Rt + Rs))*(RF/RG2) -> pass if abs(Zin_seen - Rs)/Rs <= 2% (1% parts), abs(RG2 - RG2_ideal)/RG2_ideal <= 1%, abs(G_actual - G)/G <= gain tol -> margins as fractions -> AOE-2211, AOE-2212 (Rin formula derived; book example 50/2/200 -> 60.4/226/412).
- `CHECK-fda-single-ended-output-offset`: inputs dVocm_max (V), G, input error budget (V) -> Vin_err = dVocm/G -> pass if Vin_err <= budget -> margin -> AOE-2196.
- `CHECK-fda-internal-mismatch-offset`: inputs resistor mismatch (fraction), Vocm (V), offset budget (V) -> Vos_eff = mismatch*Vocm -> pass if <= budget -> AOE-2219.
- `CHECK-fda-bias-offset`: inputs Ib, Ios (A), feedback R (ohm), matched (bool), Vos_amp (V), budget (V) -> dV = (Ios if matched else Ib)*R; total = Vos_amp + dV -> pass if total <= budget -> AOE-2200.
- `CHECK-fda-hf-cmrr-cap-match`: inputs CMRR target (dB), f (Hz), R (ohm), layout capacitance mismatch estimate (F) -> dC_max = 10^(-CMRR/20)/(2*pi*f*R) -> pass if mismatch <= dC_max -> margin = dC_max - mismatch -> AOE-2221.
- `CHECK-fda-bandwidth-at-gain`: inputs GBW_true (Hz; from response plots, not the G = 1 "bandwidth"), G (> 4), f_required (Hz), topology (VFB/CFB) -> f3dB = GBW/G for VFB (CFB ~ independent of G) -> pass if f3dB >= f_required -> margin = f3dB/f_required -> AOE-2198, AOE-2224.
- `CHECK-fda-full-power-bandwidth`: inputs slew S (V/s), peak amplitude A (V), f_signal (Hz) -> f_max = S/(2*pi*A) -> pass if f_signal <= f_max -> margin = f_max/f_signal -> AOE-2228.
- `CHECK-fda-output-headroom`: inputs Vout_max, Vout_min (V), rails (V), required headroom h (V; 0.4 V for LTC6404-class HF RRO) -> pass if Vout_max <= V+ - h and Vout_min >= V- + h -> AOE-2216.
- `CHECK-feedback-network-noise`: inputs en (V/rtHz), in (A/rtHz, at the frequency of interest incl. 1/f corner), Rf, Rg (ohm), T (K) -> en_tot = sqrt(en^2 + 4*k*T*R_eq + (in*Rf)^2) with R_eq per topology -> pass if en_tot <= budget -> AOE-2201, AOE-2232, AOE-2233.
- `CHECK-adc-driver-noise-vs-lsb`: inputs en_amp, en_R (V/rtHz), BW_eff (Hz), FS_in (V), N (bits) -> Vn = sqrt(en_amp^2 + en_R^2)*sqrt(BW_eff); LSB = FS_in/2^N -> pass if Vn <= ~1 LSB (book: "comparable ... OK") -> margin = LSB/Vn -> AOE-2208.
- `CHECK-adc-driver-distortion`: inputs HD2, HD3 (dBc at f_in, gain, load, amplitude, supply of the design), ADC bits N -> requirement e.g. <= -80 dB for 16-bit to ~100 MHz -> pass if max(HD2, HD3, THD) <= requirement -> AOE-2229, AOE-2230.
- `CHECK-transformer-adc-termination`: inputs turns ratio n, Z_src (ohm) -> R_term = n^2*Z_src (1:2 -> 200 ohm for 50 ohm) -> pass if chosen R within 1% -> AOE-2210.
- `CHECK-diffamp-cmrr-from-mismatch`: inputs ratio mismatch p (%), CMRR_required (dB) -> CMRR = 20*log10(100/p) -> pass if CMRR >= CMRR_required -> margin dB -> AOE-2235, AOE-2123.
- `CHECK-opamp-input-error-rti`: inputs Vos, TCVos, dT, Ib, Rs, Gv, budget -> V_err = (Vos + TCVos*dT) + Ib*Rs; output error = Gv*V_err -> pass if <= budget -> AOE-2236.
- `CHECK-opamp-dynamic-errors`: inputs A(f), B, fT (Hz), G_CL, f (Hz), t_settle_required (s) -> dG = 1/(1 + A*B); f3dB = fT/G_CL; tau = G_CL/(2*pi*fT); t_settle ~ 5..10*tau; phi = f/(fT/G_CL) rad -> pass per spec -> AOE-2237.

### Ch.6 Filters + Review (pp. 391-424; lines 23446-25110)
- `CHECK-filter-butterworth-order`: inputs (A_pass_dB = allowed passband droop at f_p, A_stop_dB = required attenuation at f_s, f_p Hz, f_s Hz) -> n_min = ceil( log10( (10^(A_stop/10) - 1)/(10^(A_pass/10) - 1) ) / (2*log10(f_s/f_p)) ) (derived from Eq. 6.3; reproduces the book's 19 poles for 0.1 dB / 20 dB / 1.25x) -> pass if n_design >= n_min -> margin = n_design - n_min (poles) -> source rows (AOE-2262, AOE-2264).
- `CHECK-filter-chebyshev-order`: inputs (A_pass_dB = ripple, A_stop_dB, f_p = ripple-band edge Hz, f_s Hz) -> n_min = ceil( acosh( sqrt((10^(A_stop/10) - 1)/(10^(A_pass/10) - 1)) ) / acosh(f_s/f_p) ) (derived from Eq. 6.4; reproduces the book's 8 poles) -> pass if n_design >= n_min -> margin = n_design - n_min -> source rows (AOE-2263, AOE-2264).
- `CHECK-filter-order-from-netlist`: inputs (list of reactive elements in the filter block: C count, L count; required ultimate slope dB/octave) -> n = count(C) + count(L); slope = 6*n dB/octave -> pass if slope >= required -> margin = 6*n - required (dB/octave) -> source rows (AOE-2260, AOE-2320).
- `CHECK-filter-antialias-nyquist`: inputs (fs Hz, f_pass_edge Hz, filter response H(f) from type/order/fc or from simulation, required rejection A_req dB, e.g. 6.02*n_bits for an n-bit converter) -> A1 = -20*log10(abs(H(fs/2))/abs(H(f_pass))), A2 = -20*log10(abs(H(fs - f_pass_edge))/abs(H(f_pass))) (fs - f_pass_edge is the worst-case frequency that aliases onto the band edge; 1.5 MHz in the book's 2.5 Msps / 1 MHz example) -> pass if A2 >= A_req and f_pass_edge < fs/2 -> margin = A2 - A_req (dB) -> source rows (AOE-2246, AOE-2247, AOE-2255, AOE-2310).
- `CHECK-filter-adc-dynamic-range-bits`: inputs (required dynamic range DR dB, ADC bits n) -> DR_ADC = 6*n dB -> pass if DR_ADC >= DR -> margin = 6*n - DR (dB) -> source rows (AOE-2310).
- `CHECK-filter-vcvs-section-stability`: inputs per equal-component Sallen-Key section (K) -> Q = 1/(3 - K) -> pass if K < 3 (and Q matches the Table 6.2 target) -> margin = 3 - K -> source rows (AOE-2280, AOE-2273).
- `CHECK-filter-vcvs-opamp-gbw`: inputs per section (K, section natural frequency f_sec Hz = cn*fc for Bessel/Chebyshev lowpass, fc for Butterworth, op-amp fT Hz) -> Q = 1/(3 - K); need = 50*Q^2*f_sec -> pass if fT >= need (book: approximate; alternatives 50*fc or 50*K*fc) -> margin = fT/need -> source rows (AOE-2282).
- `CHECK-filter-vcvs-resistor-range`: inputs (R per section, ohm) -> pass if 10e3 <= R <= 100e3 (typical choice; small R suffers op-amp output impedance) -> margin = min(R/10e3, 100e3/R) -> source rows (AOE-2273, AOE-2295). conf of limits: high as "typical", not absolute.
- `CHECK-filter-vcvs-gain-order`: inputs (ordered list of section gains K_i along the signal path) -> pass if K_i is non-decreasing (cascade low-gain sections first to avoid dynamic-range problems) -> margin = n/a (boolean) -> source rows (AOE-2274).
- `CHECK-filter-vcvs-rc-product`: inputs (section R ohm, C F, cn, fc Hz, filter kind LP/HP) -> target RC = 1/(2*pi*cn*fc) for LP (cn = 1 for Butterworth), cn -> 1/cn for HP -> pass if abs(RC_actual/RC_target - 1) <= tolerance budget (e.g. component tolerance) -> margin = tolerance - abs(error) -> source rows (AOE-2274, AOE-2275, AOE-2276).
- `CHECK-filter-biquad-opamp-and-loads`: inputs (G, f0 Hz, op-amp fT Hz, R_F, R_B, R_G ohm) -> need fT >= 10*G*f0 (20*G*f0 preferred) and each R >= 5e3 -> pass if both -> margin = min(fT/(10*G*f0), min(R)/5e3) -> source rows (AOE-2288).
- `CHECK-filter-biquad-design`: inputs (f0, BW, G, C) -> R_F = 1/(2*pi*f0*C), R_B = 1/(2*pi*BW*C), R_G = R_B/G; self-test: f0 = 1 kHz, BW = 20 Hz, G = 10, C = 0.01 uF -> 15.9k, 796k, 79.6k -> pass if computed parts within E96 of these -> source rows (AOE-2287, AOE-2289).
- `CHECK-filter-statevar-bp-design`: inputs (f0 Hz, Q, G) -> R_F = 5.03e7/f0, R_Q = 1e5/(3.48*Q + G - 1), R_G = 3.16e4*Q/G (ohm) -> pass if schematic values within 1% of computed -> margin = 1% - abs(error) -> source rows (AOE-2285).
- `CHECK-filter-sc-clock-alias`: inputs (f_clk Hz, passband edge f_p Hz, input prefilter response H_pre(f), required rejection A_req dB) -> band that aliases = [f_clk - f_p, f_clk + f_p]; A = -20*log10(max abs(H_pre(f)) over that band) -> pass if A >= A_req -> margin = A - A_req (dB) -> source rows (AOE-2306).
- `CHECK-filter-sc-clock-ratio`: inputs (f_clk, fc) -> ratio = f_clk/fc -> pass if ratio >= 100 preferred (50 or 25 acceptable with a sharper input anti-alias filter) -> margin = ratio/100 -> source rows (AOE-2303, AOE-2306).
- `CHECK-filter-rc-esr-floor`: inputs per RC section (R ohm, C F, ESR ohm), frequency f Hz, required attenuation A_req dB -> per-section transfer abs(Z_C)/abs(R + Z_C) with Z_C = ESR + 1/(j*2*pi*f*C); total = product over sections; floor per section ~ ESR/(R + ESR) -> pass if total attenuation at f >= A_req -> margin in dB -> source rows (AOE-2241, AOE-2242). conf medium (derived model).
- `CHECK-filter-series-lc-sine`: inputs (L H, C F, R_load ohm, f_square Hz) -> f0 = 1/(2*pi*sqrt(L*C)); X3 = 2*pi*3*f_square*L -> pass if abs(f0/f_square - 1) <= 1% and X3/R_load >> 1 (book example ratio ~38; threshold choice is low-confidence) -> margin = X3/R_load -> source rows (AOE-2243).
- `CHECK-filter-twin-t-notch`: inputs (R ohm, C F, target f_notch Hz, R/C tolerance %) -> f = 1/(2*pi*R*C) -> pass if abs(f/f_target - 1) <= tolerance allowance and components are low-TC with one trimmable -> margin = allowance - abs(error) -> source rows (AOE-2296, AOE-2297).
- `CHECK-filter-gic-frequency-limit`: inputs (op-amp fT Hz, highest frequency of use f_max Hz) -> ratio = f_max/fT -> pass if ratio <= a few percent (use 0.02-0.05; low-confidence numeric) -> margin = 0.05*fT/f_max -> source rows (AOE-2253).
- `CHECK-filter-capacitor-dielectric`: inputs (BOM rows of filter-network capacitors: dielectric) -> pass if dielectric in {C0G/NP0, polypropylene/film} for filter-defining positions; fail on X7R/X5R/Y5V (high-K), electrolytic, tantalum -> margin = count of violations -> source rows (AOE-2301, AOE-2319).
- `CHECK-filter-fir-halfband-selftest`: inputs (N, window) -> compute a_k = sinc(pi*k/2)*w(k), normalize to sum 1 -> pass if N = 8 Hamming gives [0.497374, 0.273977, 0, -0.022664, 0] to 6 decimals (verified) -> used as a unit test of the FIR generator -> source rows (AOE-2315, AOE-2316).
- `CHECK-filter-iir-onepole-coef`: inputs (tau s, t_s s) -> b = exp(-t_s/tau) -> self-test tau = 20*t_s -> b = 0.95123 -> source rows (AOE-2311, AOE-2313).
- `CHECK-filter-rc-cascade-shrinkage`: inputs (n identical buffered RC sections, desired overall f3dB) -> per-section corner f1 = f3dB/sqrt(2^(1/n) - 1) -> pass if implemented f1 within tolerance -> source rows (AOE-2239).

### Ch.7 Oscillators and Timers + Review (pp. 425-472; lines 25111-27190)
- `CHECK-timer-555-astable-period`: inputs RA (ohm), RB (ohm), C (F), f_target (Hz), tol (%) -> T = 0.693*(RA+2*RB)*C; D = (RA+RB)/(RA+2*RB) -> pass if abs(1/T - f_target)/f_target <= tol and D matches required duty (note D > 0.5 always) -> margin = tol - abs error (%) -> source rows (AOE-2329, AOE-2330)
- `CHECK-timer-cmos555-50pct`: inputs R, C, f_target -> f = 0.72/(R*C) -> pass if within tol; also require a CMOS (rail-to-rail) 555 part -> source rows (AOE-2334, AOE-2333)
- `CHECK-timer-555-bypass`: inputs timer part family (bipolar/CMOS), bypass cap present at pin (Y/N), distance to Vcc pin (mm) -> pass if bypass present adjacent to chip for any 555, mandatory for bipolar (150 mA transition spikes) -> source rows (AOE-2332)
- `CHECK-osc-ltc1799-freq`: inputs R_SET (kohm), N (1,10,100), f_target -> f = 100/(N*R_SET) MHz -> pass if within tol and R_SET inside datasheet solid-line region; margin = f error vs +/-0.5% typ accuracy -> source rows (AOE-2339)
- `CHECK-timer-ltc6991-period`: inputs R_SET (kohm, 50-800), k (0-7), T_target -> T = 2^(3k)*(R_SET/50)*1.024e-3 s; V_DIV = V+*(2k+1)/32 -> pass if T within tol and R_SET in 50-800 k; divider resistors 1% -> source rows (AOE-2341, AOE-2342)
- `CHECK-osc-accuracy-vs-resolution`: inputs oscillator spec accuracy (%), required accuracy (%) -> pass if spec accuracy (not resolution/step) <= requirement -> source rows (AOE-2344, AOE-2328)
- `CHECK-osc-schmitt-inverter-use`: inputs oscillator topology, required frequency tolerance (%) -> fail if topology = Schmitt-inverter RC and tolerance < 50% -> source rows (AOE-2328)
- `CHECK-osc-wien-startup`: inputs JFET g_m,min (S), resistance the JFET must supply at Vgs=0 (ohm) -> pass if 1/g_m,min < required R_on (i.e., R_on,max below the critical add-on) -> source rows (AOE-2353)
- `CHECK-osc-sine-from-square-filter`: inputs f0, duty (50% or not), f_c, filter order -> pass if f0 < f_c < 2*f0 (or 3*f0 at 50% duty) -> source rows (AOE-2348, AOE-2349)
- `CHECK-osc-wien-frequency`: inputs R (ohm), C (F), f_target (Hz), gain network ratio -> f = 1/(2*pi*R*C); require nominal loop gain 3.00 with an amplitude-control element present -> pass if f within tol and gain element present -> source rows (AOE-2350, AOE-2351)
- `CHECK-osc-crystal-load-cap`: inputs C1, C2 (pF), C_stray (pF, pins + traces), CL_spec (pF), C0 (pF), C1m (motional, pF) -> CL_eff = C1*C2/(C1+C2) + C_stray; offset_ppm = 1e6*C1m/2*(1/(C0+CL_eff) - 1/(C0+CL_spec)) -> pass if abs(offset_ppm) <= allowed initial-tolerance share -> margin = allowed - abs(offset_ppm) -> source rows (AOE-2367, AOE-2368, AOE-2373)
- `CHECK-osc-crystal-cap-stability`: inputs dCL (pF drift over temperature/aging of load caps and strays), C0, CL, C1m -> d(ppm) = 1e6*C1m*dCL/(2*(C0+CL)^2) -> pass if <= stability budget (book anchor: 0.002 pF <-> 0.1 ppm at 10 MHz, CL = 30 pF) -> source rows (AOE-2369)
- `CHECK-osc-crystal-mode-match`: inputs IC oscillator mode (series/parallel), crystal cut/spec mode, crystal Rs_max vs IC max Rs, CL spec vs IC recommendation -> pass if modes equal and Rs_max(crystal) <= Rs_max(IC) -> source rows (AOE-2367, AOE-2370, AOE-2376, AOE-2377)
- `CHECK-osc-pierce-series-R`: inputs f_osc (Hz), C2 (F), R2 (ohm) -> X_C2 = 1/(2*pi*f_osc*C2) -> report ratio R2/X_C2 (book: "comparable"; anchors ~330 k at 32 kHz, ~1 k at 5 MHz) -> flag if ratio outside user band -> source rows (AOE-2373)
- `CHECK-osc-timekeeping-drift`: inputs initial tol (ppm), temp stability (ppm), aging (ppm/day or per year), mission time (days) -> worst drift_s = 86400*days*(sum of ppm terms)*1e-6 (aging integrated) -> pass if <= requirement -> source rows (AOE-2374, AOE-2381, AOE-2382)
- `CHECK-osc-class-vs-requirement`: inputs required stability (fractional), oscillator class -> class stability from Table 7.2 (RC 1e-2..1e-3, ceramic 1e-2..1e-3, LC 1e-3..1e-5, XO 1e-5, TCXO 1e-6..1e-7, OCXO better, atomic 1e-10..1e-14) -> pass if class stability <= requirement -> source rows (AOE-2365, AOE-2379, AOE-2381, AOE-2383)
- `CHECK-osc-jitter-threshold`: inputs v_n_sig, v_n_th (V), slew S (V/s) or (f, V0) or (V_step, t_r), jitter budget (s) -> dt = (v_n_sig + v_n_th)/S; S = 2*pi*f*V0 or V_step/t_r -> pass if dt <= budget -> margin = budget - dt -> source rows (AOE-2392)
- `CHECK-osc-dds-setting`: inputs f_clk (Hz), n (accumulator bits), f_target, f_out_max (part), LPF corner -> FTW = round(f_target*2^n/f_clk); f_act = FTW*f_clk/2^n; resolution = f_clk/2^n -> pass if abs(f_act - f_target) <= tol, f_target <= f_out_max (AD9912: 400 MHz at 1 GHz clock), and LPF passes f_target while attenuating clock/step products -> source rows (AOE-2384)
- `CHECK-osc-pll-step`: inputs f_ref, r, n, required step -> f_out = (n/r)*f_ref; step = f_ref/r -> pass if step <= required and f_out inside VCO range -> source rows (AOE-2385)
- `CHECK-osc-varactor-bias`: inputs V_bias_min (V), oscillation amplitude across varactor (Vpk), pair topology (single / back-to-back) -> pass if V_bias_min > 1 V and Vpk << V_bias_min (or back-to-back pair used) -> source rows (AOE-2360, AOE-2361)
- `CHECK-timer-oneshot-width`: inputs vendor K, R, C, t_min, target width, tolerance -> t_w = K*R*C + t_min; worst case = t_w*(1 +/- 0.10 unit-to-unit +/- few % temp/supply) -> pass if window inside tolerance and vendor pinned in BOM (K differs 0.45 vs 1.0 across vendors) -> source rows (AOE-2403, AOE-2404)
- `CHECK-timer-oneshot-trigger`: inputs trigger width (ns), part min trigger width at V+ (ns), retrigger interval, part retrigger recovery -> pass if trigger width >= min and interval >= recovery -> source rows (AOE-2400, AOE-2402)
- `CHECK-timer-oneshot-components`: inputs C (pF/uF), R (ohm), part lower threshold VL/V+, diode across R (Y/N), capacitor dielectric -> flag if C <= 1000 pF and VL/V+ not near ground; fail if R > 10 Mohm; if C in uF range require leakage review, non-high-k dielectric, and diode across R -> source rows (AOE-2405, AOE-2406)
- `CHECK-timer-oneshot-supply-isolation`: inputs monostable present (Y/N), private RC-filtered or regulated V+ (Y/N), output drives cable/off-board (Y/N), buffer present (Y/N) -> pass if filtered supply present and every off-board output buffered -> source rows (AOE-2407, AOE-2409)
- `CHECK-timer-pulse-limiter`: inputs load pulse current, continuous rating, drive source (software/MCU?), T1 (s), T2 (s), max safe on-time, max safe duty -> require hardware limiter when pulse current > continuous rating and source is software; pass if T1 <= max safe on-time and T1/T2 <= max safe duty -> source rows (AOE-2411)
- `CHECK-timer-counter-delay`: inputs f_osc nominal and tolerance (%), counter bits k or decode N, required delay and tolerance, trim present (Y/N) -> t = 2^k/f_osc (or N/f_osc) -> pass if tolerance band inside requirement; if RC oscillator, require trimmer or crystal source -> source rows (AOE-2413, AOE-2415, AOE-2343)
- `CHECK-timer-timerblox-reset`: inputs RST source, RST state at t_init (~1.7 ms), POR length -> fail if RST can be asserted at end of init (first period skipped) -> source rows (AOE-2414)
- `CHECK-hwfw-gpio-load`: inputs load current (mA), pin rating (mA), pins paralleled -> pass if load <= pins*rating (book: 2 x 20 mA for 40 mA relay) -> source rows (AOE-2420)

### Ch.8 §8.1-§8.8: noise fundamentals, BJT/JFET low-noise design (pp. 473-521; lines 27191-30489)
- `CHECK-noise-johnson-rms`: inputs (R ohm, T_C degC, B Hz) -> vn = sqrt(4*1.380649e-23*(T_C+273.16)*R*B) -> pass if vn <= noise_budget_V -> margin = 20*log10(noise_budget_V/vn) dB -> source rows (AOE-2423, AOE-2424, AOE-2426)
- `CHECK-noise-shot-rms`: inputs (Idc A, B Hz) -> in = sqrt(2*1.60e-19*Idc*B) -> pass if in <= current_noise_budget -> margin = 20*log10(budget/in) dB; flag if path is a metallic conductor or transistor current source (formula overestimates) -> source rows (AOE-2430, AOE-2432)
- `CHECK-noise-enbw`: inputs (filter_type in {rc1, bw2, avg}, f3dB Hz or T s) -> B = 1.57*f3dB (rc1), 1.11*f3dB (bw2), 1/(2T) (avg) -> used as B in other checks; pass if B >= signal bandwidth (no signal truncation) -> margin = B/B_signal -> source rows (AOE-2437, AOE-2438, AOE-2439)
- `CHECK-noise-amp-rti-total`: inputs (en V/sqrt(Hz), in A/sqrt(Hz), Rs ohm, T K, B Hz, G) -> vn_in = sqrt(en^2 + (in*Rs)^2 + 4kT*Rs)*sqrt(B); vn_out = G*vn_in -> pass if 20*log10(vs/vn_in) >= SNR_min -> margin = SNR - SNR_min dB -> source rows (AOE-2422, AOE-2441, AOE-2442, AOE-2448)
- `CHECK-noise-nf-at-source`: inputs (en, in, Rs, T) -> NF = 10*log10(1 + (en^2 + (in*Rs)^2)/(4kT*Rs)) (drop the 4 for matched 50-ohm RF) -> pass if NF <= NF_max -> margin = NF_max - NF dB -> source rows (AOE-2443, AOE-2445, AOE-2448)
- `CHECK-noise-nf-tn-convert`: inputs (NF dB or Tn K, T = 293 K) -> Tn = T*(10^(NF/10) - 1); NF = 10*log10(Tn/T + 1) -> pass if round-trip consistent and Tn <= Tn_max -> margin = Tn_max - Tn -> source rows (AOE-2447)
- `CHECK-noise-snr-from-nf`: inputs (vs V rms, Rs, B, NF dB) -> SNR = 10*log10(vs^2/(4kT*Rs*B)) - NF -> pass if SNR >= SNR_min -> margin = SNR - SNR_min -> source rows (AOE-2446)
- `CHECK-noise-bjt-en`: inputs (Ic A, rbb' ohm, T K) -> en = sqrt(4kT*(kT/(2*q*Ic) + rbb')) -> pass if en <= en_target -> margin = en_target/en -> source rows (AOE-2451)
- `CHECK-noise-bjt-en-full`: inputs (Ic A, beta0, rbb' ohm, fci Hz, f Hz, T K) -> re = kT/(q*Ic); Ib = Ic/beta0; en^2 = 2*q*Ic*re^2 + 4kT*rbb' + 2*q*Ib*rbb'^2*(1 + fci/f) -> pass if en(f_min) <= en_target -> margin = en_target/en -> source rows (AOE-2463, AOE-2457)
- `CHECK-noise-bjt-in-full`: inputs (Ic, beta0, fci, fT, f) -> in^2 = 2*q*(Ic/beta0)*(1 + fci/f) + 2*q*Ic*(f/fT)^2 -> pass if in*Rs <= en (or budget) -> margin = en/(in*Rs) -> source rows (AOE-2458)
- `CHECK-noise-bjt-ic-optimum`: inputs (Rs ohm, table of Ic vs en(Ic), in(Ic) at signal f) -> ea^2(Ic) = en^2 + (in*Rs)^2; Ic_opt = argmin ea -> pass if ea(Ic_chosen) <= (1 + tol_design)*ea_min (tol_design project-set; book gives no tolerance) -> margin = ea_min/ea(Ic_chosen) -> source rows (AOE-2460, AOE-2477)
- `CHECK-noise-ac-coupling-cap`: inputs (in A/sqrt(Hz) incl. 1/f at f_min, f_min Hz, C F, en V/sqrt(Hz)) -> vn_C = in/(2*pi*f_min*C) -> pass if vn_C <= k_design*en (k_design is a project-set fraction; the book only says "small enough") -> margin = en/vn_C -> source rows (AOE-2462)
- `CHECK-noise-current-source-suppression`: inputs (Ic A, V_Re V) -> reduction_dB = 10*log10(50 mV/V_Re) (valid for V_Re >= 50 mV) -> pass if collector noise current (in_shot*sqrt(50mV/V_Re)) <= budget -> margin = budget/in_col -> source rows (AOE-2470, AOE-2471)
- `CHECK-noise-en-from-nf`: inputs (NF dB, Rs ohm, T K) -> en = sqrt(4kT*Rs)*sqrt(10^(NF/10) - 1) -> pass if NF read >= 1 dB (reliability) and Rs small enough that in*Rs << en -> margin = NF - 1 dB -> source rows (AOE-2472, AOE-2474)
- `CHECK-noise-1f-corner`: inputs (enL at fL, enH) -> fc = fL*((enL/enH)^2 - 1); en(f) = enH*sqrt(1 + fc/f) -> pass if fc <= f_min_signal (white-dominated band) -> margin = f_min_signal/fc -> source rows (AOE-2435, AOE-2475)
- `CHECK-noise-rn-match`: inputs (en V/sqrt(Hz), in A/sqrt(Hz), Rs ohm, T) -> Rn = en/in; NF_min = 10*log10(1 + en*in/(2kT)); NF(Rs) per eq 8.31 -> pass if NF(Rs) <= NF_max; report Rs/Rn so reviewers see how far the design sits from the noise match (book gives no tolerance) -> margin = NF_max - NF(Rs) -> source rows (AOE-2478, AOE-2480, AOE-2481)
- `CHECK-noise-base-current-at-source`: inputs (Ic A, beta_min, Rs or abs(Zs) ohm, en) -> in = sqrt(2*q*Ic/beta_min); ratio = in*abs(Zs)/en -> pass if total ea = sqrt(en^2 + (in*abs(Zs))^2) <= ea_budget -> margin = ea_budget/ea -> source rows (AOE-2496, AOE-2499, AOE-2500)
- `CHECK-noise-bjt-ic-opt-ceiling`: inputs (rbb' ohm, beta_min, T) -> Ic(o) ~ VT/(2.5*rbb'); en(o) = 1.5*sqrt(4kT*rbb'); in(o) = sqrt(2*q*Ic(o)/beta_min); Rn(o) = en(o)/in(o) -> pass if design Ic <= Ic(o) when Rs > Rn(o) (current noise dominates) -> margin = Ic(o)/Ic -> source rows (AOE-2505, AOE-2506)
- `CHECK-noise-parallel-devices`: inputs (N devices, rbb' or gm per device, Ic per device, Cin per device, C_source) -> BJT: rbb'_eff = rbb'/N, re_eff = re/N; JFET at same total Id: en x N^(-1/4); Cin_total = N*Cin -> pass if en target met and Cin_total <= C_source (JFET rule) and emitter ballast either >= 50 mV drop or batch Vbe-matched -> margin = C_source/Cin_total -> source rows (AOE-2483, AOE-2509, AOE-2512, AOE-2518)
- `CHECK-noise-jfet-en`: inputs (gm S at operating Id, T) -> en_min = sqrt(4kT*2/(3*gm)); design value = en_min*(1.1..1.3), worst 2x (book's measured spread) -> pass if 2*en_min <= en_budget (worst case) -> margin = en_budget/(2*en_min) -> source rows (AOE-2516, AOE-2517)
- `CHECK-noise-jfet-in`: inputs (Ig A at operating Tj and VDG, Rs or abs(Zs), en) -> in = sqrt(3.2e-19*Ig); v = in*abs(Zs) -> pass if v <= en (or budget); note crossover Rs_x = 4kT/in^2 -> margin = en/v -> source rows (AOE-2522)
- `CHECK-noise-jfet-hf-current`: inputs (en, Cin F, f_max Hz, in_dc) -> in_hf = en*2*pi*f_max*Cin -> pass if sqrt(in_dc^2 + in_hf^2)*abs(Zs) <= budget -> margin -> source rows (AOE-2449, AOE-2523)
- `CHECK-noise-diff-pair-en`: inputs (en_single, in_single, per-device Ic) -> en_diff = sqrt(2)*en_single; in_diff = in_single -> used downstream; pass if en_diff <= en_budget -> margin = en_budget/en_diff -> source rows (AOE-2485, AOE-2534)
- `CHECK-noise-noninv-amp-rti`: inputs (en_diff, in, R1, R2, Rs, T, B) -> R_par = R1*R2/(R1+R2); eA^2 = en^2 + 4kT*R_par + (in*R_par)^2; iA = in; e^2 = eA^2 + (Rs*iA)^2 + 4kT*Rs; vn = sqrt(e^2*B) -> pass if vn <= budget/1.4 when correlation unknown (book: RSS may err by up to 1.4x) -> margin = budget/(1.4*vn) -> source rows (AOE-2535)
- `CHECK-noise-inv-amp-current`: inputs (in, R2, T) -> iA^2 = in^2 + 4kT/R2 -> pass if iA*(Rs + R1) <= current-noise budget -> margin -> source rows (AOE-2537; full inverting-amplifier noise formula in the §8.9 piece)
- `CHECK-noise-tia-stability`: inputs (Rf ohm, Cf F, Cin F, fT_openloop Hz) -> f_RfCf = 1/(2*pi*Rf*Cf); f_RfCin = 1/(2*pi*Rf*Cin); required fT > f_RfCf^2/f_RfCin -> pass if fT_openloop >= required -> margin = fT_openloop/required (book example: 25 MHz vs 7 MHz) -> source rows (AOE-2490)
- `CHECK-noise-tia-input-current`: inputs (Rf, Rbias, Ib of input device, en_total, Cin, BW) -> in_R = sqrt(4kT/Rf + 4kT/Rbias); in_b = sqrt(2*q*Ib); in_C(f) = en*2*pi*f*Cin; in_rms = sqrt(integral over BW of (in_R^2 + in_b^2 + in_C(f)^2) df) -> pass if in_rms <= signal_min/SNR_min and >= photodiode leakage shot noise (no over-grooming) -> margin -> source rows (AOE-2489, AOE-2491, AOE-2492)

### Ch.8 §8.9-§8.10: op-amp circuit noise, low-noise op-amp selection, signal transformers (pp. 521-537; lines 30490-33317)
Constants: k = 1.38e-23 J/K, q = 1.602e-19 C, T = 300 K unless the design states otherwise.

- `CHECK-opnoise-total-input-density`: inputs (en nV/rtHz, in pA/rtHz, Rs ohm, T K, en_spec nV/rtHz; all at the frequency of interest) -> en_tot = sqrt(en^2 + (in*Rs)^2 + 4*k*T*Rs) -> pass if en_tot <= en_spec -> margin_dB = 20*log10(en_spec/en_tot) -> source rows (AOE-2549, AOE-2577).
- `CHECK-opnoise-best-part-for-Rs`: inputs (candidate table: part, en, in at f; Rs ohm; parts already filtered for non-noise constraints per AOE-2577) -> en_tot(part) per CHECK-opnoise-total-input-density; NF_dB(part) = 10*log10(en_tot^2/(4*k*T*Rs)) -> pass if the selected part has the minimum en_tot among candidates -> margin = en_tot(best)/en_tot(selected) (1.0 = optimal) -> source rows (AOE-2550, AOE-2577, AOE-2579).
- `CHECK-opnoise-rn-dominance`: inputs (en, in, Rs_seen ohm = impedance at op-amp inputs incl. feedback network and source) -> Rn = en/in -> pass (en-dominant design intent) if Rs_seen <= Rn/5 -> margin = Rn/(5*Rs_seen) (>= 1 passes; Rn/30 is the conservative end) -> source rows (AOE-2558, AOE-2540).
- `CHECK-opnoise-inverting-stage-rti`: inputs (en, in, R1 ohm, R2 ohm, T, e_spec) -> e_in = sqrt(en^2*(1+R1/R2)^2 + 4*k*T*R1*(1+R1/R2) + (in*R1)^2); e_out = e_in*R2/R1 -> pass if e_in <= e_spec -> margin_dB = 20*log10(e_spec/e_in) -> source rows (AOE-2537).
- `CHECK-opnoise-integrated-vn`: inputs (enH nV/rtHz white level, fc Hz, f1 Hz, f2 Hz, Vn_spec uV rms or Vpp_spec uV) -> Vn = enH*sqrt((f2 - f1) + fc*ln(f2/f1)); for f1 = 0.1 Hz, f2 = 10 Hz also Vpp_est = 6*Vn -> pass if Vn <= Vn_spec (and Vpp_est <= Vpp_spec when given) -> margin_dB = 20*log10(spec/value) -> source rows (AOE-2566, AOE-2569).
- `CHECK-opnoise-1f-relevance`: inputs (fc Hz, BW Hz = upper band edge, f_min Hz) -> flag = (BW < 10*fc) -> pass if flag is false, or if CHECK-opnoise-integrated-vn was run with the 1/f term and en evaluated at f_min -> margin = BW/(10*fc) -> source rows (AOE-2565, AOE-2580).
- `CHECK-opnoise-in-source`: inputs (datasheet in A/rtHz or blank, bias_cancel flag, Ib_max(Tj) A) -> if bias_cancel: in_used = datasheet in (fail if blank; shot-noise estimate underestimates 10x-40x); else in_used = max(datasheet in, sqrt(2*q*Ib_max(Tj))) -> pass if in_used is defined and was evaluated at worst-case Tj -> margin n/a -> source rows (AOE-2555, AOE-2561, AOE-2563).
- `CHECK-opnoise-ib-spread`: inputs (Ib_typ, Ib_max, input technology BJT/JFET/CMOS, Ib value used in design calcs) -> ratio = Ib_max/Ib_typ -> pass if design used Ib_max (at operating Tj and Vcm); flag BJT parts with ratio > 3 as datasheet anomalies -> margin = Ib_budget/Ib_max -> source rows (AOE-2561, AOE-2562, AOE-2556).
- `CHECK-opnoise-encin-fom`: inputs (en nV/rtHz, Cin pF (larger of CM/diff), Cext pF, f_max Hz) -> FOM = en*(Cin + Cext) nV*pF; in_C(f_max) = en*2*pi*f_max*(Cin + Cext) -> pass if the selected part has the lowest FOM among candidates meeting other constraints -> margin = FOM(best)/FOM(selected) -> source rows (AOE-2571, AOE-2572, AOE-2578, AOE-2574).
- `CHECK-opnoise-psrr-supply-noise`: inputs (per rail: supply noise density Vs(f) V/rtHz or ripple V, PSRR_rail(f) dB; en_tot of the stage) -> Vn_psrr_RTI(f) = sqrt(sum over rails of (Vs_rail(f)*10^(-PSRR_rail(f)/20))^2) -> pass if Vn_psrr_RTI(f) <= en_tot(f) at every in-band f (criterion is a design choice; the book gives no threshold) -> margin_dB = 20*log10(en_tot/Vn_psrr_RTI) -> source rows (AOE-2575, AOE-2576).
- `CHECK-opnoise-transformer-match`: inputs (Zs ohm, n turns ratio, R_w ohm total winding resistance referred to secondary, en, in, NF_spec dB) -> Z_seen = n^2*Zs + R_w; NF_dB = 10*log10((4*k*T*Z_seen + en^2 + (in*Z_seen)^2)/(4*k*T*n^2*Zs)); guide n_opt ~ sqrt((en/in)/Zs) (derived: puts n^2*Zs at Rn) -> pass if NF_dB <= NF_spec -> margin = NF_spec - NF_dB -> source rows (AOE-2594, AOE-2595, AOE-2597).
- `CHECK-opnoise-hybrid-min-gain`: inputs (closed-loop gain Gcl, front-end voltage gain G1, second stage unity-gain stable yes/no, series R+C compensation present yes/no) -> pass if Gcl >= G1 or compensation present -> margin = Gcl/G1 -> source rows (AOE-2585).
- `CHECK-opnoise-cascade-rti`: inputs (en1 of front end or single device, number of paralleled devices N, differential yes/no, G1, en2 of second stage) -> en_front = en1*(sqrt(2) if differential else 1)/sqrt(N); en_RTI = sqrt(en_front^2 + (en2/G1)^2) -> pass if en_RTI <= en_spec -> margin_dB = 20*log10(en_spec/en_RTI) -> source rows (AOE-2586, AOE-2590, AOE-2600).
- `CHECK-opnoise-bjt-diff-protection`: inputs (front-end type, worst-case differential input voltage Vdiff_max V, back-to-back diodes present) -> fail if type = BJT and Vdiff_max > 5 V and no diodes -> margin = 5 - Vdiff_max (V) -> source rows (AOE-2591).
- `CHECK-opnoise-self-heating`: inputs (P_diss W, theta_JA C/W, Ta_inbox C) -> Tj = Ta_inbox + P_diss*theta_JA; re-evaluate Ib and in at Tj -> pass if Ib(Tj) and in(Tj) meet budget -> margin = budget/value -> source rows (AOE-2564, AOE-2563).
- `CHECK-opnoise-bom-dual-technology-mpn`: inputs (BOM: MPN, manufacturer, noise-critical flag) -> fail if MPN in {LMV358, LMV321} (or other known BJT/CMOS dual-source numbers) and manufacturer is blank on a noise-critical line -> source rows (AOE-2545).

### Ch.8 §8.11-§8.16 + Review: transimpedance amplifiers, noise measurement, supply noise, interference/shielding/grounding (pp. 537-593; lines 33318-35540)
- `CHECK-tia-bandwidth`: inputs (Rf ohm, Cin_total F, GBW Hz, BW_req Hz) -> fRC = 1/(2*pi*Rf*Cin); fc = sqrt(fRC*GBW) -> pass if fc >= BW_req -> margin = fc/BW_req - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2606, AOE-2620).
- `CHECK-tia-cf-stability`: inputs (Rf ohm, Cf F, Cin_max F, fT Hz) -> fGM = sqrt(fT/(2*pi*Rf*Cin_max)); fc = 1/(2*pi*Rf*Cf) -> pass if fc <= fGM (damping ratio >= ~0.7 at worst-case Cin) -> margin = fGM/fc - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2615, AOE-2618).
- `CHECK-tia-rf-shot-limit`: inputs (Iin_min_dc A, Rf ohm) -> Vdrop = Iin*Rf -> pass if Vdrop >= 0.100 V (Rf noise below signal shot noise; equality at 0.050 V) -> margin = Vdrop/0.100 - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2619).
- `CHECK-tia-noise-crossover`: inputs (Rf, en V/rtHz, Cin F, T K, BW Hz) -> fx = sqrt(4kT/Rf)/(2*pi*en*Cin) -> report enC-dominated if fx < BW; flag need for output lowpass and low en*Cin op-amp -> margin = fx/BW - 1 (negative = enC dominated) -> source rows (AOE-2612, AOE-2614, AOE-2621).
- `CHECK-tia-output-swing`: inputs (Iin_max A incl. dc, Rf ohm, Vswing_max V) -> Vout = Iin_max*Rf -> pass if Vout <= Vswing_max -> margin = Vswing_max/Vout - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2623).
- `CHECK-tia-output-lowpass`: inputs (fc Hz, f3dB_LP Hz or none) -> pass if an output lowpass exists and fc <= f3dB_LP <= 2*fc (the "2x" ceiling is a low-confidence quantification of "slightly above") -> margin = 2*fc/f3dB_LP - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2621).
- `CHECK-tia-inoise-hot`: inputs (Ib(T_max) A) -> in = sqrt(2*q*Ib(T_max)) -> pass if in (RSS with other terms) meets the input-noise requirement at T_max -> margin = in_req/in_total - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2608, AOE-2609).
- `CHECK-tia-bootstrap-noise`: inputs (en_amp, en_JFET V/rtHz, C_det F, bootstrap reduction factor N (>= 10), f Hz) -> Cin_eff = C_det/N; in = 2*pi*f*sqrt((en_amp*Cin_eff)^2 + (en_JFET*C_det)^2) -> pass if in <= in_req at f -> margin = in_req/in - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2636, AOE-2637).
- `CHECK-tia-cascode-rolloff`: inputs (Iq A, Cin_eff F, f_B Hz, fT(Iq) Hz) -> re = 0.025/Iq; f_in = 1/(2*pi*re*Cin_eff); diverted = f_B/fT -> pass if f_in > f_B and diverted <= 0.1 (10% figure from text example; threshold low-confidence) -> margin = f_in/f_B - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2640, AOE-2641, AOE-2646).
- `CHECK-tia-summing-node-resistors`: inputs (list of resistors R_i tied to the summing junction, Rf) -> in_R = sqrt(4kT*sum(1/R_i)) -> pass if each R_i >= Rf (no resistor contributes more Johnson current than Rf) -> margin = min(R_i)/Rf - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2642, AOE-2646).
- `CHECK-tia-integrator-reset`: inputs (Cf F, Iin_max A, Vsat V, t_reset s) -> t_max = Vsat*Cf/Iin_max -> pass if t_reset < t_max -> margin = t_max/t_reset - 1 -> source rows (AOE-2650).
